that def/theorem/lemma, and check whether the prose says NEITHER MORE NOR LESS than the Lean.
You are hunting for: prose STRONGER than the Lean (overclaim), WEAKER (underclaim), citing the
WRONG lemma, omitting a HYPOTHESIS, or misstating a QUANTIFIER (inclusive-or vs exclusive-or,
"at least one" vs "exactly one", <= vs <, forall vs exists, "the cut form of Theorem X" when the
Lean proof does not actually invoke theorem X). Plausibility is irrelevant and is exactly the
failure mode to catch.

 succeeded in 0ms:
    47	\section{Definitions}\label{sec:defs}
    48	
    49	Fix a linearly ordered vertex set $V$. A simplex is a finite subset $s\subseteq V$; if
    50	$\card{s}=k+1$ we call it a $k$-simplex. A \emph{chain} is a finitely supported function
    51	$M$ from simplices to $\Z$ (\code{Chain V}), written $M=\sum_s M(s)\,s$, with orientations
    52	fixed by the vertex order. The boundary $\bd$ is the usual signed face operator
    53	(\code{bdry}); $\bd\bd=0$. The \emph{norm} is $\norm{M}=\sum_s\card{M(s)}$ (\code{nrm}); a
    54	chain is \emph{simplicial} if every coefficient lies in $\{-1,0,1\}$
    55	(\code{SimplicialChain}), and then $\norm{M}$ is the number of simplices in its support.
    56	
    57	\begin{definition}[Filling volume; taut filling]\label{def:taut}
    58	For a cycle $X$ (a chain with $\bd X=0$), the \emph{filling volume} is
    59	\[
    60	\Zvol(X)=\min\{\norm{M}:\bd M=X\}\qquad(\code{Zvol}).
    61	\]
    62	A chain $M$ is a \emph{taut filling} of $X$ if $\bd M=X$ and $\norm{M}=\Zvol(X)$, i.e.\ $M$
    63	is a norm-minimal filling of its own boundary (\code{IsTaut}).
    64	\end{definition}
    65	
    66	Tautness contributes exactly three things to the proofs, all consequences of minimality:
    67	\begin{itemize}
    68	\item[(T1)] \emph{Boundary control:} $\bd M=X$ holds on the nose.
    69	\item[(T2)] \emph{Purity / no junk:} the generators of a taut filling of an $n$-cycle are
    70	all $(n{+}1)$-simplices (\code{IsTaut.dim\_pure}), and every vertex of $M$ lies on the
    71	boundary (\code{IsTaut.vert\_subset}, via \code{IsTaut.no\_internal\_vertex}).
    72	\item[(T3)] \emph{No extraneous cancellation:} a taut chain contains no complete cone
    73	$y\ast L$ over a cycle $L$ (\code{not\_taut\_complete\_cone}); such a subchain could be
    74	refilled with strictly fewer simplices.
    75	\end{itemize}
    76	``Taut'' is therefore purely combinatorial $\ell^1$-minimality with these structural
    77	corollaries; it is not a metric or curvature condition.
    78	
    79	\begin{definition}[Combinatorial $2$-sphere]\label{def:sphere}
    80	A set $\sigma$ of triangles is a \emph{combinatorial $2$-sphere} (\code{IsSphere2}) if it is
    81	pure $2$-dimensional, every edge lies in exactly two triangles, every vertex link is a
    82	connected cycle, the $1$-skeleton is connected, and $V+F=E+2$. These properties are taken
    83	as the definition.
    84	\end{definition}
    85	
    86	\begin{definition}[AlmostDisjoint]\label{def:ad}
    87	Fix $n\ge 1$ and $A,B\subseteq V$. Closed pure $n$-cycles $X,Y$ are
    88	\emph{AlmostDisjoint over $(A,B)$} if every generator of $X$ is an $(n{+}1)$-subset of $A$,
    89	every generator of $Y$ is an $(n{+}1)$-subset of $B$, $\bd X=\bd Y=0$, and
    90	\[
    91	\card{A\cap B}\ \le\ n+1 .
    92	\]
    93	Mathematically: the two regions overlap in at most an $n$-simplex's worth of vertices.
    94	This is the \emph{entire} separation hypothesis --- no metric, no topological transversality.
    95	(Informal name; in Lean it is the hypothesis bundle of
    96	\code{Zvol\_add\_of\_almost\_disjoint\_full} and \code{IsTaut.splits\_full}.)
    97	\end{definition}
    98	
    99	For the stickerball theorem we build complexes of tetrahedra one at a time. Let $\tau$ be a
   100	set of tetrahedra ($4$-element simplices) with current boundary $B$ (a set of triangles).
   101	
   102	\begin{definition}[Clean glue step; clean shelling]\label{def:clean}
   103	Adding a tetrahedron $t$ to $\tau$ is a \emph{clean glue step} (\code{CleanGlueStep}) if:
   104	\begin{itemize}
   105	\item[(a)] $t$ meets the current boundary $B$ in one or two of its four triangular faces,
   106	and the new boundary is $B$ updated by symmetric difference with the faces of $t$;
   107	\item[(b)] $t\notin\tau$ and no already-interior face of $t$ is rogue (each old face of $t$
   108	lies in a shared boundary triangle); and the three \emph{link certificates}:
   109	\item[(c1)] each triangle of $t$ lies in at most one tetrahedron of $\tau$ (so at most two
   110	after adding $t$);
   111	\item[(c2)] each edge of $t$ is new to $\tau$, or shares an apex with $\tau$'s edge-link there;
   112	\item[(c3)] each vertex of $t$ is new to $\tau$, or shares an apex with $\tau$'s vertex-link there.
   113	\end{itemize}
   114	A \emph{clean shelling} of $\tau$ with boundary $B$ is an ordering $t_1,\dots,t_k$ of $\tau$
   115	in which $t_1$ is a single tetrahedron and each $t_{i+1}$ is added to $\{t_1,\dots,t_i\}$ by
   116	a clean glue step, terminating at boundary $B$ (\code{IsCleanShelling},
   117	\code{CleanShellFrom}).
   118	\end{definition}
   119	
   120	The certificates (c1)--(c3) are precisely the hypotheses needed to preserve, across one
   121	insertion, the three conditions defining a \emph{clean $3$-complex}.
   122	
   123	\begin{definition}[Clean $3$-complex]\label{def:clean3}
   124	A set $\tau$ of tetrahedra is a \emph{clean $3$-complex} (\code{IsClean3Complex}) if it is
   125	pure ($3$-dimensional), triangle-bounded (every triangle in at most two tetrahedra), and
   126	\emph{normal}: both its edge-links and its vertex-links are connected.
   127	\end{definition}
   128	
   129	\begin{definition}[Stickerball; root; RootedStickerball; AnyRootedStickerball]\label{def:sb}
   130	A set $\tau$ of tetrahedra is a \emph{stickerball} with boundary $B$ if it admits a clean
   131	shelling ending at $B$ and $B\neq\varnothing$ (\code{IsStickerball}).
   132	A \emph{root} of a stickerball is a tetrahedron $t\in\tau$ at which some clean shelling can
   133	\emph{begin}. The stickerball is \emph{rooted at $t$} (a \emph{RootedStickerball}) if $t$ is
   134	a root, and is an \emph{AnyRootedStickerball} if every tetrahedron of $\tau$ is a root
   135	(\code{IsAnyrootedStickerball} $=$ \code{IsStickerball} together with
   136	\code{FreelyCleanShellable}, where \code{FreelyCleanShellable} asserts a clean shelling
   137	beginning at each $t\in\tau$).
   138	\end{definition}
   139	
   140	\begin{remark}
   141	``RootedStickerball'' and ``AnyRootedStickerball'' are stated in Lean-backed terms. The
   142	clean shelling certifies normality (\S\ref{sec:classical}); we do not silently upgrade
   143	``stickerball'' to ``shellable ball'' or ``$3$-ball'' --- the proved bridge and the missing
   144	bridge are stated in \S\ref{sec:classical}.
   145	\end{remark}
   146	
   147	%==========================================================================
   148	\section{Splitting under AlmostDisjoint}\label{sec:split}
   149	
   150	The engine of the whole development is a single splitting theorem: a taut filling of a sum
   151	of AlmostDisjoint cycles is the sum of taut fillings of the summands. We first record the
   152	homological input and the small-support triviality that drive it.
   153	
   154	\subsection*{The homological input}
   155	
   156	\begin{lemma}[$H_1=0$ and the cut]\label{lem:cut}
   157	On a combinatorial $2$-sphere, over $\F_2$, every $1$-cycle is a boundary:
   158	$\operatorname{im}\bd_2=\ker\bd_1$ (\code{range\_bd2\_eq\_ker\_bd1}). Consequently, if
   159	$\gamma$ is a triple of vertices whose three edges lie in $\sigma$ but which is not a face
   160	of $\sigma$, then the $1$-cycle of its edges bounds a set $W$ of faces of $\sigma$
   161	(\code{exists\_cut}).
   162	\end{lemma}
   163	
   164	\begin{proof}
   165	The $\F_2$ $2$-cycles are spanned by the all-faces chain (dual-graph connectivity), so
   166	$\dim\operatorname{im}\bd_2=F-1$; the connected $1$-skeleton gives
   167	$\dim\operatorname{im}\bd_1=V-1$, hence $\dim\ker\bd_1=E-(V-1)$, which equals $F-1$ by
   168	$V+F=E+2$. With $\operatorname{im}\bd_2\subseteq\ker\bd_1$ this forces equality. A non-face
   169	triple $\gamma$ has each vertex on exactly two of its edges, so its edge-chain is a
   170	$1$-cycle and therefore a boundary $\bd_2 W$.
   171	\end{proof}
   172	
   173	\begin{lemma}[Small-support triviality]\label{lem:smallsupp}
   174	A closed chain whose generators all have $n+1$ vertices, supported on a set of at most
   175	$n+1$ vertices, is zero (\code{eq\_zero\_of\_closed\_supp\_card\_eq}).
   176	\end{lemma}
   177	
   178	\begin{proof}
   179	On at most $n+1$ vertices there is at most one $(n{+}1)$-subset, so the chain is $c\cdot s$
   180	for a single simplex; $\bd(c\,s)=0$ forces $c=0$.
   181	\end{proof}
   182	
   183	\subsection*{The split}
   184	
   185	Fix a common vertex $p\in A\cap B$. There is a norm-non-increasing \emph{kill operator}
   186	$K_{A,p}$ (\code{Projection.lean}, a cone projection toward $p$) with: $K_{A,p}$ fixes every
   187	chain confined to $A$ (\code{Kmap\_eq\_self}); and $K_{A,p}$ annihilates every closed pure
   188	$n$-cycle confined to $B$ (\code{Kmap\_eq\_zero\_of\_closed}), because its image is a closed
   189	pure cycle on $A\cap B$, hence on $\le n+1$ vertices, hence zero by
   190	Lemma~\ref{lem:smallsupp}. Symmetrically for $K_{B,q}$ with $q\in A\cap B$, $q\neq p$.
   191	
   192	\begin{theorem}[Additivity]\label{thm:add}
   193	If $X,Y$ are AlmostDisjoint over $(A,B)$ with $n\ge 1$, then
   194	$\Zvol(X+Y)=\Zvol(X)+\Zvol(Y)$ (\code{Zvol\_add\_of\_almost\_disjoint\_full}).
   195	\end{theorem}
   196	
   197	\begin{proof}
   198	$(\le)$ Concatenating minimal fillings of $X$ and $Y$ fills $X+Y$ with no norm cancellation,
   199	since the supports meet only in the small set $A\cap B$.
   200	$(\ge)$ Let $M$ be a minimal (hence taut) filling of $X+Y$, so $\Zvol(X{+}Y)=\norm M$; its
   201	generators are pure $(n{+}2)$-simplices lying in $A\cup B$ (\code{IsTaut.dim\_pure},
   202	\code{IsTaut.vert\_subset}). Each generator $t$ splits as
   203	$(t\cap A\cap B)\sqcup(t\setminus A)\sqcup(t\setminus B)$; as $\card{A\cap B}\le n+1<n+2$,
   204	$t$ cannot avoid both $t\setminus A$ and $t\setminus B$, so $t$ is killed by \emph{at least
   205	one} of $K_{A,p},K_{B,q}$ (\code{Kkills\_or\_Kkills}, an inclusive ``or''). Equivalently the
   206	two retained sets are disjoint --- a generator may be killed by both (retained by neither),
   207	e.g.\ one with $\card{t\setminus A}=\card{t\setminus B}=2$, and that is harmless. With the
   208	per-map bound $\norm{K_{A,p}M}+\norm{M{\restriction}_{\mathrm{kill}_A}}\le\norm M$
   209	(\code{nrm\_Kmap\_add\_killed\_le}) and
   210	$\norm M\le\norm{M{\restriction}_{\mathrm{kill}_A}}+\norm{M{\restriction}_{\mathrm{kill}_B}}$
   211	(\code{nrm\_le\_killed\_add\_killed}) this gives $\norm{K_{A,p}M}+\norm{K_{B,q}M}\le\norm{M}$.
   212	Because $K_{A,p}$ fixes the $A$-part of $\bd M$ and kills its closed $B$-part, $K_{A,p}M$ fills
   213	$X$ and $K_{B,q}M$ fills $Y$; thus $\norm{M}\ge\Zvol X+\Zvol Y$. (Exclusivity --- each generator
   214	killed by \emph{exactly} one --- is not used here; it holds only later, for the taut filling in
   215	Theorem~\ref{thm:split}, once \code{no\_double\_kill} rules out double kills.)
   216	\end{proof}
   217	
   218	\begin{theorem}[Splitting under AlmostDisjoint]\label{thm:split}
   219	Let $X,Y$ be AlmostDisjoint over $(A,B)$ with $n\ge 2$, and let $M$ be a taut filling of
   220	$X+Y$. Then, writing $M_A=M\!\restriction_{\,\subseteq A}$ and
   221	$M_B=M\!\restriction_{\,\not\subseteq A}$,
   222	\[
   223	M=M_A+M_B,\qquad \bd M_A=X,\qquad \bd M_B=Y,
   224	\]
   225	and $M_A,M_B$ are themselves taut (\code{IsTaut.splits\_full}).
   226	\end{theorem}
   227	
   228	\begin{proof}
   229	Tautness enters precisely here, turning Theorem~\ref{thm:add} into rigidity. Since $M$ is
   230	taut, $\norm{M}=\Zvol(X+Y)=\Zvol X+\Zvol Y$, while
   231	$\norm{M}\ge\norm{K_{A,p}M}+\norm{K_{B,q}M}\ge\Zvol X+\Zvol Y$. So all inequalities are
   232	equalities, with two consequences.
   233	
   234	\emph{No double kill} (\code{no\_double\_kill}): if some generator were retained by neither
   235	operator, the middle sum would be strictly smaller than $\norm M$. Hence every generator is
   236	retained by exactly one side, and $K_{A,p}M,K_{B,q}M$ are \emph{taut} fillings of $X,Y$.
   237	
   238	\emph{No straddling simplex} (\code{no\_extreme\_hybrid}). The three-part decomposition
   239	leaves, besides pure generators ($\subseteq A$ or $\subseteq B$), only ``extreme hybrids'':
   240	a single vertex $y_0$ on the far side and none in $A\cap B$. If such hybrids existed,
   241	collect those through a fixed $y_0$; their faces opposite $y_0$ form a nonempty cycle $L$
   242	(closed by the same $\F_2$-parity behind Lemma~\ref{lem:cut}), so $M$ would contain the
   243	complete cone $y_0\ast L$ --- contradicting (T3). This is the only place $n\ge 2$ is used:
   244	the opposite faces need $\ge 2$ vertices for $L$ to be a genuine cycle.
   245	
   246	Thus every generator of $M$ is $\subseteq A$ or $\subseteq B$, so the literal restriction
   247	gives $M=M_A+M_B$, and $M_A,M_B$ coincide with $K_{A,p}M,K_{B,q}M$; the boundary identities
   248	and the tautness of the pieces follow.
   249	\end{proof}
   250	
   251	\begin{remark}[Why exactly AlmostDisjoint]
   252	The proof uses the separation hypothesis only through Lemma~\ref{lem:smallsupp}, which needs
   253	nothing beyond $\card{A\cap B}\le n+1$. There is no appeal to any geometric or topological
   254	transversality; AlmostDisjointness is the exact combinatorial content that makes the cross
   255	terms vanish.
   256	\end{remark}
   257	
   258	The rational analogue (\code{Qvol\_add\_of\_almost\_disjoint\_full},
   259	\code{IsQTaut.splits\_full}) follows by clearing denominators, applying
   260	Theorems~\ref{thm:add}--\ref{thm:split} over $\Z$, and rescaling.
   261	
   262	%==========================================================================
   263	\section{Taut fillings are AnyRootedStickerballs}\label{sec:main}
   264	
   265	We now prove the main theorem by induction on the number of tetrahedra, using
   266	Theorem~\ref{thm:split} (in its $2$-sphere ``cut'' form) to produce the smaller taut
   267	fillings. First, the engine that gives the filling its $3$-dimensional shape.
   268	
   269	\begin{theorem}[Taut fillings are pure and triangle-bounded]\label{thm:pm}
   270	Let $\sigma$ be a combinatorial $2$-sphere, $X$ the $\pm1$ cycle on $\sigma$
   271	(\code{UnitOn X $\sigma$}), and $M$ a taut simplicial filling with $\bd M=X$. Then every
   272	generator of $M$ is a tetrahedron and every triangle lies in at most two of them
   273	(\code{taut\_isPseudomanifold}; purity from \code{IsTaut.dim\_pure}).
   274	\end{theorem}
   275	
   276	\begin{proof}
   277	Purity is (T2): $X$ is $2$-dimensional, so a taut filling is $3$-dimensional.
   278	Triangle-boundedness is a strong induction on the number of tetrahedra, by the trichotomy
   279	used again below. \emph{Base} ($\sigma$ has $\le 4$ vertices): $M$ is a single tetrahedron.
   280	\emph{Degree-$3$ vertex}: cut its star (Lemma~\ref{lem:cut}), apply the inductive
   281	hypothesis to the smaller filling, re-glue. \emph{No degree-$3$ vertex}: take two disjoint
   282	tetrahedra $e,u$ each meeting the boundary in exactly two faces
   283	(\code{aleph\_disjoint\_eligible\_pair}), remove $e$, apply the inductive hypothesis, re-glue;
   284	the key point is the \emph{triangle rule-out} (\code{faceCount\_removeTet\_sharedFace\_eq\_zero}):
   285	a shared face of $e$ cannot reappear after removing $e$, since the disjoint witness $u$ would
   286	otherwise force that triangle's incidence to $3$ in the inductively triangle-bounded filling
   287	$M-u$.
   288	\end{proof}
   289	
   290	\begin{theorem}[Main]\label{thm:anyrooted}
   291	Let $\sigma$ be a combinatorial $2$-sphere, $X$ the $\pm1$ cycle on $\sigma$, and $M$ a taut
   292	simplicial filling with $\bd M=X$. Then the set of tetrahedra of $M$ is an
   293	\emph{AnyRootedStickerball} with boundary $\sigma$
   294	(\code{taut\_filling\_is\_anyrootedStickerball}).
   295	\end{theorem}
   296	
   297	By Theorem~\ref{thm:pm} the support consists of tetrahedra; $\sigma\neq\varnothing$ gives
   298	nonempty boundary. The content is that every tetrahedron is a root:
   299	
   300	\begin{theorem}\label{thm:freely}
   301	Under the hypotheses of Theorem~\ref{thm:anyrooted}, for every tetrahedron $t_\star$ of $M$
   302	there is a clean shelling of $M$ beginning with $t_\star$ (\code{theorem3\_clean}, via the
   303	induction skeleton \code{theorem3\_core\_clean} with cases \code{base\_free\_clean},
   304	\code{deg3\_step\_clean}, \code{prime\_step\_clean}).
   305	\end{theorem}
   306	
   307	\begin{proof}
   308	Strong induction on the number $N$ of tetrahedra of $M$. The inductive invariant is exactly
   309	the AnyRooted statement: a clean shelling beginning at \emph{any} prescribed $t_\star$. The
   310	cases are a trichotomy on $\sigma$. In each non-base case the cut (Lemma~\ref{lem:cut})
   311	supplies strictly smaller taut fillings of smaller spheres to which the inductive hypothesis
   312	applies --- by Theorem~\ref{thm:split} itself in the degree-$3$ case, and by a separate
   313	side-decomposition resting on the same cut in the prime case.
   314	
   315	\medskip
   316	\noindent\textbf{Base ($\sigma$ has $\le 4$ vertices).} Then $\sigma$ is a tetrahedron
   317	boundary and $M$ is a single tetrahedron $T$; the one-term shelling starts at $T$ and is
   318	vacuously clean.
   319	
   320	\medskip
   321	\noindent\textbf{Degree-$3$ vertex $v$.} The link of $v$ is a triangle $\gamma$, and the
   322	star of $v$ is one tetrahedron $T=v\ast\gamma$. Since $\card{\sigma}\ge 5$, $\gamma$ is a
   323	non-face triangle whose edges lie in $\sigma$, so by Lemma~\ref{lem:cut} replacing the three
   324	triangles at $v$ by $\gamma$ yields a $2$-sphere $\sigma'$ with one fewer vertex. Restricting
   325	$M$ to the non-star side is a \emph{direct instance of Theorem~\ref{thm:split}}, with the two
   326	capped vertex-sets as $A,B$ (their overlap is exactly $\gamma$, so $\card{A\cap B}=n{+}1=3$):
   327	the lemma \code{taut\_splits\_for\_capped\_cut} invokes \code{IsTaut.splits} verbatim. It
   328	produces a \emph{taut} filling $M'$ of $\sigma'$ with strictly fewer tetrahedra, so by
   329	induction $M'$ is an AnyRootedStickerball. Two facts let $T$ be glued cleanly: $\gamma$ is a boundary
   330	triangle of $M'$ lying in a \emph{unique} remainder tetrahedron $t_0$ (tautness pins it,
   331	$\text{faceCount}(\gamma)=1$; \code{degree3\_hanchor}), and $v$ is a vertex absent from $M'$
   332	(\code{degree3\_apex\_notMem\_*}), so adjoining $T$ along the single face $\gamma$ satisfies
   333	(a)--(c3) (the apex $v$ is new, so its link certificates are vacuous). Now assemble, given
   334	$t_\star$:
   335	\begin{itemize}
   336	\item if $t_\star\neq T$: take a clean shelling of $M'$ rooted at $t_\star$ (induction) and
   337	append $T$ last;
   338	\item if $t_\star=T$: start with $T$, then a clean shelling of $M'$ rooted at the anchor
   339	$t_0$ (induction), which glues to $T$ along $\gamma$.
   340	\end{itemize}
   341	Either way $M$ has a clean shelling rooted at $t_\star$.
   342	
   343	\medskip
   344	\noindent\textbf{No degree-$3$ vertex ($\card{\sigma}\ge 5$).} Pick a tetrahedron $e$ of $M$
   345	with exactly two faces on $\sigma$ (its existence is a count: with no degree-$3$ vertex there
   346	are more boundary triangles than tetrahedra; \code{exists\_eligibleTet}). Let $f_3,f_4$ be its
   347	two interior faces and $f_3\cap f_4$ its \emph{flip edge}. Remove $e$.
   348	\begin{itemize}
   349	\item \emph{Flip edge absent.} Exposing $f_3,f_4$ is a $2$--$2$ flip of the boundary: the new
   350	boundary is again a $2$-sphere $\sigma'$ on the same vertices
   351	(\code{isSphere2\_flipBoundary\_of\_eligible}) and $M-e$ is a taut filling of it with fewer
   352	tetrahedra. By induction $M-e$ is an AnyRootedStickerball; re-gluing $e$ (two shared faces)
   353	is a clean glue step, its shared faces clean by the rule-out of Theorem~\ref{thm:pm}.
   354	\item \emph{Flip edge present.} Then $f_3\cap f_4\in\sigma$, and $\sigma$ separates along this
   355	edge into two $2$-spheres $\sigma_1,\sigma_2$; correspondingly $M-e$ splits along this edge into
   356	two taut sub-fillings $M_1,M_2$ with fewer tetrahedra (\code{flipEdgePresent\_side\_sets},
   357	\code{flipEdgePresent\_side\_algebra}). This is a decomposition of the same filter-by-side shape
   358	as Theorem~\ref{thm:split}, but a \emph{separate} argument: it is built directly on the cut
   359	(Lemma~\ref{lem:cut}/\code{exists\_cut}), not through \code{IsTaut.splits}.
   360	By induction each is an AnyRootedStickerball; concatenate their clean shellings --- rooting
   361	the second at the tetrahedron adjacent to the bridge --- and insert $e$ to bridge them
   362	(\code{glueStep\_bridge\_left}, \code{glueStep\_bridge\_right}).
   363	\end{itemize}
   364	In both sub-cases, choosing the root of the smaller piece(s) appropriately (and, if
   365	$t_\star=e$, starting with $e$) yields a clean shelling of $M$ rooted at $t_\star$.
   366	
   367	\medskip
   368	This proves the inductive invariant for $M$, completing the induction.
   369	\end{proof}
   370	
   371	\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:anyrooted}]
   372	Theorem~\ref{thm:freely} gives a clean shelling rooted at each tetrahedron; with nonempty
   373	boundary this is the AnyRootedStickerball conclusion. Each re-gluing above is a clean glue
   374	step, so the link certificates (c1)--(c3) hold at every step; the assembled shelling is
   375	therefore a genuine clean shelling and the rooted structure is preserved through the
   376	assembly.
   377	\end{proof}
   378	
   379	\begin{remark}[Where each ingredient is used]
   380	The recursion always lands on strictly smaller taut fillings of strictly smaller spheres,
   381	so the induction terminates. Tautness is used (i) to make every cut-restriction again taut,
   382	so the inductive hypothesis applies (Theorem~\ref{thm:split} for the degree-$3$ cut, the
   383	analogous side-decomposition for the prime case); (ii) through
   384	Theorem~\ref{thm:pm} to keep every intermediate complex triangle-bounded, which the prime
   385	rule-out needs; and (iii) to pin boundary triangles into unique tetrahedra, giving the
   386	gluing anchors. The rooted structure is preserved because every assembly step is a
   387	\emph{clean} glue step.
   388	\end{remark}
   389

   413	
   414	\noindent\emph{Definitions weaker than their classical namesakes.}
   415	\begin{itemize}
   416	\item \emph{Taut} (\code{IsTaut}) is combinatorial $\ell^1$-minimality
   417	(Definition~\ref{def:taut}), not a geometric condition.
   418	\item \emph{Triangle-bounded} (\code{IsPseudomanifold} in the development) means only ``every
   419	triangle in at most two tetrahedra.'' It is \emph{not} a full pseudomanifold; normality
   420	(link-connectedness) is established separately, via the clean shelling (see
   421	\S\ref{sec:classical}). Theorem~\ref{thm:pm} proves the triangle bound only.
   422	\item \emph{Clean shelling} (\code{IsCleanShelling}) carries the link certificates
   423	(c1)--(c3) as data; this is what makes it certify a clean $3$-complex, and is stronger than a
   424	boundary-only shelling.
   425	\end{itemize}
   426	
   427	%==========================================================================
   428	\section{Relation to the classical statement; remaining bridges}\label{sec:classical}
   429	
   430	The conclusion of Theorem~\ref{thm:anyrooted} is stated in Lean-backed terms
   431	(AnyRootedStickerball). Two questions separate this from a classical ``shellable normal
   432	$3$-ball'' statement.
   433	
   434	\paragraph{Proved bridge: AnyRootedStickerball $\Rightarrow$ normal $3$-pseudomanifold.}
   435	The development proves that a stickerball is a clean $3$-complex:
   436	\[
   437	\code{IsStickerball.isClean3Complex}\colon\quad
   438	\text{stickerball}\ \Longrightarrow\ \text{pure}\ \wedge\ \text{triangle-bounded}\ \wedge\
   439	\text{Normal3},
   440	\]
   441	where \code{Normal3} is \emph{edge-link connected and vertex-link connected}
   442	(\code{IsAnyrootedStickerball.isClean3Complex} for the AnyRooted form). This is proved by
   443	iterating the single-insertion lemma \code{clean3Complex\_insert} along the clean shelling;
   444	the three certificates (c1)--(c3) of each glue step supply exactly the triangle-count,
   445	edge-link, and vertex-link preservation (\code{isPseudomanifold\_insert},
   446	\code{edgeLinkConnected\_insert}, \code{vertexLinkConnected\_insert}). Thus pseudomanifoldness
   447	(triangle bound), edge-link connectedness, vertex-link connectedness, and nonempty boundary
   448	are all \emph{Lean-proved} consequences of the conclusion; the corresponding corollaries are
   449	\code{taut\_filling\_is\_clean3Complex} and \code{taut\_filling\_is\_shellable}.
   450	
   451	\paragraph{Missing bridge: stickerball $\Rightarrow$ topological/PL $3$-ball.}
   452	The statement ``a clean-shellable normal $3$-pseudomanifold with nonempty boundary is
   453	(PL-)homeomorphic to the $3$-ball'' is the classical shelling theorem of Danaraj--Klee /
   454	Bj\"orner. \emph{No bridge theorem from \code{IsStickerball} to a topological or PL ball
   455	appears in the current development}; it is recorded only as a docstring remark. Any classical
   456	reading of Theorem~\ref{thm:anyrooted} as ``$M$ triangulates a $3$-ball'' must invoke that
   457	external theorem explicitly. In the present, fully formalized terms the conclusion is exactly:
   458	\emph{the tetrahedra of a taut filling of a combinatorial $2$-sphere form an
   459	AnyRootedStickerball --- a clean-shellable normal $3$-pseudomanifold, with boundary the given
   460	sphere, that can be clean-shelled starting from any tetrahedron}.
   461	
   462	\paragraph{Hypotheses to carry into a paper statement.}
   463	Chains are simplicial and the boundary cycle is $\pm1$ on $\sigma$
   464	(\code{SimplicialChain}, \code{UnitOn}); splitting (Theorem~\ref{thm:split}) needs dimension
   465	$n\ge 2$ while additivity (Theorem~\ref{thm:add}) needs only $n\ge 1$; AlmostDisjointness is
   466	$\card{A\cap B}\le n+1$; the ambient $V$ is infinite; and \code{IsSphere2} includes the Euler
   467	relation $V+F=E+2$ as part of its definition, from which $H_1=0$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:cut}) is
   468	derived rather than assumed.
   469

    82	/-! ## Combinatorial 2-spheres -/
    83	
    84	/-- A combinatorial closed surface: pure 2-dimensional, every edge in exactly
    85	two faces, connected vertex links, connected. This is `IsSphere2` minus the
    86	`χ = 2` constraint — the geometric content the homology machinery (b₂ = 1,
    87	r₁ = V−1) actually rests on. Euler enters `IsSphere2` only via nonemptiness. -/
    88	structure IsClosedSurface (σ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop where
    89	  pure : ∀ f ∈ σ, f.card = 3
    90	  closed : ∀ e ∈ edgesOf σ, edgeDeg σ e = 2
    91	  linkConn : ∀ v ∈ vertsOf σ, ConnOn (linkGraph σ v) (linkVerts σ v)
    92	  conn : ConnOn (skel σ) (vertsOf σ)
    93	
    94	/-- A combinatorial 2-sphere: a closed surface with Euler characteristic 2.
    95	This is the paper's "clean complex" (normal pseudomanifold) sharpened by
    96	`χ = 2`, replacing "simplicial triangulation of S²". -/
    97	structure IsSphere2 (σ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop where
    98	  pure : ∀ f ∈ σ, f.card = 3
    99	  closed : ∀ e ∈ edgesOf σ, edgeDeg σ e = 2
   100	  linkConn : ∀ v ∈ vertsOf σ, ConnOn (linkGraph σ v) (linkVerts σ v)
   101	  conn : ConnOn (skel σ) (vertsOf σ)
   102	  euler : (vertsOf σ).card + σ.card = (edgesOf σ).card + 2
   103	
   104	def IsSphere2.toClosedSurface {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsSphere2 σ) :
   105	    IsClosedSurface σ :=
   106	  ⟨h.pure, h.closed, h.linkConn, h.conn⟩
   107

   109	
   110	theorem three_mul_card_faces {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsSphere2 σ) :
   111	    3 * σ.card = 2 * (edgesOf σ).card := by
   112	  classical
   113	  have hfilter : ∀ f ∈ σ, (edgesOf σ).filter (fun e => e ⊆ f)
   114	      = f.powersetCard 2 := by
   115	    intro f hf

    30	boundary triangle), it carries the *quantitative* clean-insert certificates that
    31	`clean3Complex_insert` consumes.  These do **not** follow from `clean` alone, so

    33	structure CleanGlueStep (t : Finset V) (τ B B' : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop where
    34	  /-- the underlying boundary glue step (one or two shared faces, symmetric difference). -/
    35	  weak : BoundaryGlueStep t B B'
    36	  /-- no rogue old face: every face of `t` already in `τ` lies in a shared boundary triangle. -/
    37	  clean : ∀ f, f ⊆ t → (∃ s ∈ τ, f ⊆ s) → ∃ g ∈ tetFaces t ∩ B, f ⊆ g
    38	  /-- `t` is fresh (rules out a doubled tet). -/
    39	  newTet : t ∉ τ
    40	  /-- each triangle of `t` lies in ≤ 1 old tet (so triangle-count stays ≤ 2). -/
    41	  hpmc : ∀ f, f.card = 3 → f ⊆ t → faceCount τ f ≤ 1
    42	  /-- each edge of `t` is new to `τ` or already shares an apex with `τ`'s edge-link there. -/
    43	  helc : ∀ e, e ⊆ t → e.card = 2 →
    44	    edgeLinkVerts τ e = ∅ ∨ ((t \ e) ∩ edgeLinkVerts τ e).Nonempty
    45	  /-- each vertex of `t` is new to `τ` or already shares an apex with `τ`'s vertex-link there. -/
    46	  hvlc : ∀ v ∈ t, vertexLinkVerts τ v = ∅ ∨
    47	    ((t \ {v}) ∩ vertexLinkVerts τ v).Nonempty
    48	
    49	/-- Clean shelling from boundary `B₀`, **threading the accumulated tet-set `τ`**:
    50	each step glues onto the set built so far, then recurses on `insert t τ`. -/
    51	def CleanShellFrom :
    52	    Finset (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → List (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → Prop
    53	  | _, B₀, [],     B => B = B₀
    54	  | τ, B₀, t :: l, B => ∃ B₁, CleanGlueStep t τ B₀ B₁ ∧ CleanShellFrom (insert t τ) B₁ l B
    55	
    56	/-- A clean shelling with final boundary `B`: the head tet is the initial
    57	tetrahedron (accumulated set `{t}`, boundary `tetFaces t`, no glue step), and the
    58	rest are glued on cleanly in order. -/
    59	def IsCleanShelling : List (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → Prop
    60	  | [],     _ => False
    61	  | t :: l, B => t.card = 4 ∧ CleanShellFrom {t} (tetFaces t) l B
    62	
    63	/-- A *clean ball*: a tet-set admitting a clean shelling (a nodup ordering of its
    64	tets gluing up cleanly to boundary `B`). -/
    65	def IsCleanBall (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    66	  ∃ l : List (Finset V), l.toFinset = τ ∧ l.Nodup ∧ IsCleanShelling l B
    67	
    68	/-- *Freely clean shellable*: **any** tet can serve as the head of a clean
    69	shelling.  This is the public free-sticker-ball predicate Theorem 3 exports. -/
    70	def FreelyCleanShellable (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    71	  ∀ t ∈ τ, ∃ l : List (Finset V),
    72	    l.head? = some t ∧ l.toFinset = τ ∧ l.Nodup ∧ IsCleanShelling l B
    73	
    74	/-! ## Stickerball predicates (the combinatorial B³ certificate) -/
    75	
    76	/-- A **stickerball**: a shellable clean 3-complex with nonempty boundary.  Concretely `IsCleanBall`
    77	(a clean shelling exists — building *up* by sticking tetrahedra on, the reverse of shucking down) with
    78	a nonempty boundary `B`.  Being an `IsCleanBall` it is in particular an `IsClean3Complex`
    79	(pure, every triangle in ≤ 2 tets, connected vertex *and* edge links) — see `IsStickerball.isClean3Complex`.
    80	
    81	By the standard PL theorem that a shellable normal 3-pseudomanifold with nonempty boundary is a PL
    82	ball, this is the combinatorial certificate for a triangulation of `B³`.  This project proves the
    83	combinatorial certificate; it does **not** formalize that external PL-homeomorphism theorem (see
    84	`notes/future-projects.md`). -/
    85	def IsStickerball (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    86	  IsCleanBall τ B ∧ B.Nonempty
    87	
    88	/-- An **anyrooted stickerball** is a stickerball whose shelling may start with any prescribed
    89	tetrahedron (`FreelyCleanShellable`).  "Stickerball" by itself is *not* anyrooted — that is the
    90	strengthened property. -/
    91	def IsAnyrootedStickerball (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    92	  IsStickerball τ B ∧ FreelyCleanShellable τ B
    93	
    94	lemma IsAnyrootedStickerball.toStickerball {τ B : Finset (Finset V)}
    95	    (h : IsAnyrootedStickerball τ B) : IsStickerball τ B := h.1
    96	
    97	lemma IsAnyrootedStickerball.freelyCleanShellable {τ B : Finset (Finset V)}
    98	    (h : IsAnyrootedStickerball τ B) : FreelyCleanShellable τ B := h.2
    99

   323	/-! ## Theorem 1, part 1: additivity of Zvol -/

   325	lemma exists_fill_eq_Zvol {X : Chain V} (W : Chain V) (hW : bdry W = X) :
   326	    ∃ M : Chain V, bdry M = X ∧ nrm M = Zvol X := by
   327	  obtain ⟨M, h1, h2⟩ :=
   328	    Nat.sInf_mem (⟨nrm W, W, hW, rfl⟩ :
   329	      {m | ∃ M : Chain V, bdry M = X ∧ nrm M = m}.Nonempty)
   330	  exact ⟨M, h1, h2⟩
   331	
   332	/-- The mass of `M` is at most the mass killed by `K_{A,p}` plus the mass
   333	killed by `K_{B,q}`, when every generator is killed on at least one side. -/
   334	lemma nrm_le_killed_add_killed {A B : Finset V} {p q : V} {M : Chain V}
   335	    (hkill : ∀ t ∈ M.support, Kkills A p t ∨ Kkills B q t) :
   336	    nrm M ≤ nrm (M.filter (Kkills A p)) + nrm (M.filter (Kkills B q)) := by
   337	  classical
   338	  have hsubA : (M.filter (Kkills A p)).support ⊆ M.support := by
   339	    rw [Finsupp.support_filter]; exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
   340	  have hsubB : (M.filter (Kkills B q)).support ⊆ M.support := by
   341	    rw [Finsupp.support_filter]; exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
   342	  rw [nrm_eq_sum_subset hsubA, nrm_eq_sum_subset hsubB,
   343	    ← Finset.sum_add_distrib, nrm]
   344	  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun s hs => ?_
   345	  rw [Finsupp.filter_apply, Finsupp.filter_apply]
   346	  rcases hkill s hs with h | h
   347	  · rw [if_pos h]
   348	    omega
   349	  · rw [if_pos h]
   350	    split <;> omega
   351	
   352	/-- **Theorem 1, additivity** (Ellison): for an almost disjoint union
   353	`X + Y` — `X` a closed pure `n`-chain on `A`, `Y` on `B`, with
   354	`(A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1` witnessed by two distinct shared vertices
   355	`p ≠ q ∈ A ∩ B` — the filling volume adds:
   356	`Zvol (X + Y) = Zvol X + Zvol Y`. -/
   357	theorem Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint {A B : Finset V} {p q : V} {n : ℕ}
   358	    (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   359	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   360	    {X Y : Chain V}
   361	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   362	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   363	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0) :
   364	    Zvol (X + Y) = Zvol X + Zvol Y := by
   365	  have hpA : p ∈ A := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp).1
   366	  have hqB : q ∈ B := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq).2
   367	  have hXYc : bdry (X + Y) = 0 := by rw [map_add, hXc, hYc, add_zero]
   368	  obtain ⟨Mx, hMx, hMxn⟩ := exists_fill_eq_Zvol (cone p X) (bdry_cone_of_closed hXc)
   369	  obtain ⟨My, hMy, hMyn⟩ := exists_fill_eq_Zvol (cone p Y) (bdry_cone_of_closed hYc)
   370	  obtain ⟨M, hM1, hM2⟩ :=
   371	    exists_fill_eq_Zvol (cone p (X + Y)) (bdry_cone_of_closed hXYc)
   372	  refine le_antisymm ?_ ?_
   373	  · have hbd : bdry (Mx + My) = X + Y := by rw [map_add, hMx, hMy]
   374	    calc Zvol (X + Y) ≤ nrm (Mx + My) := Zvol_le hbd
   375	      _ ≤ nrm Mx + nrm My := nrm_add_le _ _
   376	      _ = Zvol X + Zvol Y := by rw [hMxn, hMyn]
   377	  · have hMt : IsTaut M := by rw [IsTaut, hM1]; exact hM2
   378	    have hbdsupp : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = n + 1 := by
   379	      intro s hs
   380	      rw [hM1] at hs
   381	      rcases Finset.mem_union.mp (Finsupp.support_add hs) with h | h
   382	      · exact (hX s h).2
   383	      · exact (hY s h).2
   384	    have hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2 :=
   385	      fun s hs => hMt.dim_pure hbdsupp s hs
   386	    have hdim2 : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card := by
   387	      intro s hs
   388	      rw [hpure s hs]
   389	      omega
   390	    have hvert : vert M ⊆ A ∪ B := by
   391	      refine (hMt.vert_subset hdim2).trans ?_
   392	      rw [hM1]
   393	      refine (vert_add_subset X Y).trans ?_
   394	      exact Finset.union_subset_union
   395	        (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hX s hs).1)
   396	        (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hY s hs).1)
   397	    have hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B :=
   398	      fun t ht => (supp_subset_vert ht).trans hvert
   399	    have hbx : bdry (Kmap A p M) = X := by
   400	      rw [bdry_Kmap A hpA, hM1, map_add,
   401	        Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (hX s hs).1),
   402	        Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn hp hC hY hYc, add_zero]
   403	    have hby : bdry (Kmap B q M) = Y := by
   404	      rw [bdry_Kmap B hqB, hM1, map_add,
   405	        Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (hY s hs).1),
   406	        Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hq)
   407	          (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hC) hX hXc, zero_add]
   408	    have h1 := nrm_Kmap_add_killed_le A p M
   409	    have h2 := nrm_Kmap_add_killed_le B q M
   410	    have h3 : nrm M ≤ nrm (M.filter (Kkills A p)) + nrm (M.filter (Kkills B q)) :=
   411	      nrm_le_killed_add_killed fun t ht =>
   412	        Kkills_or_Kkills hp hq hpq hC (hsuppAB t ht) (hpure t ht)
   413	    have h4 : Zvol X ≤ nrm (Kmap A p M) := Zvol_le hbx
   414	    have h5 : Zvol Y ≤ nrm (Kmap B q M) := Zvol_le hby
   415	    omega
   416	
   417	/-- **Theorem 1, additivity (full paper statement)**: the hypothesis
   418	`(A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1` alone (with `n ≥ 1`) suffices — no witnessing pair
   419	`p ≠ q` is needed.  When `|A ∩ B| ≥ 2` we extract a pair and invoke
   420	`Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint`; when `|A ∩ B| ≤ 1` we enlarge both `A` and
   421	`B` by the same fresh vertices to reach exactly `|A' ∩ B'| = 2` (still
   422	`≤ n + 1`), which never changes `X`, `Y`, or the conclusion.
   423	
   424	`[Infinite V]` is pure ambient bookkeeping, not a finiteness restriction: chains are
   425	finitely supported (`Chain V := Finset V →₀ ℤ`), so the active vertex set is always finite.
   426	The hypothesis only guarantees that *fresh cut vertices are available* for the `|A ∩ B| ≤ 1`
   427	enlargement above (the paper's "add a brand-new point to `C` if necessary"); a taut filling
   428	never uses vertices beyond its boundary (`IsTaut.vert_subset`), so the unused ambient vertices
   429	play no mathematical role. -/
   430	theorem Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint_full {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V] [Infinite V]
   431	    {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   432	    {X Y : Chain V}
   433	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   434	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   435	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0) :
   436	    Zvol (X + Y) = Zvol X + Zvol Y := by
   437	  classical
   438	  by_cases h2 : 2 ≤ (A ∩ B).card
   439	  · obtain ⟨p, hp, q, hq, hpq⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.mp h2
   440	    exact Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc
   441	  · push_neg at h2
   442	    -- `|A ∩ B| ≤ 1`: enlarge both sides by `F`, a fresh set capping the
   443	    -- intersection to exactly two shared vertices.
   444	    have hle : (A ∪ B).card ≤ (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := Nat.le_add_right _ _
   445	    obtain ⟨W, hWsub, hWcard⟩ :=
   446	      Infinite.exists_superset_card_eq (A ∪ B) _ hle
   447	    set F := W \ (A ∪ B) with hF
   448	    have hFdisj : Disjoint F (A ∪ B) := Finset.sdiff_disjoint
   449	    have hFcard : F.card = 2 - (A ∩ B).card := by
   450	      have hkey : F.card + (A ∪ B).card = (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := by
   451	        rw [hF, Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card hWsub, hWcard]
   452	      omega
   453	    set A' := A ∪ F with hA'
   454	    set B' := B ∪ F with hB'
   455	    have hFA : Disjoint F A := hFdisj.mono_right Finset.subset_union_left
   456	    have hFB : Disjoint F B := hFdisj.mono_right Finset.subset_union_right
   457	    have hinter : A' ∩ B' = (A ∩ B) ∪ F := by
   458	      ext x
   459	      simp only [hA', hB', Finset.mem_inter, Finset.mem_union]
   460	      constructor
   461	      · rintro ⟨hxA | hxF, hxB | hxF⟩
   462	        · exact Or.inl ⟨hxA, hxB⟩
   463	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   464	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   465	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   466	      · rintro (⟨hxA, hxB⟩ | hxF)
   467	        · exact ⟨Or.inl hxA, Or.inl hxB⟩
   468	        · exact ⟨Or.inr hxF, Or.inr hxF⟩
   469	    have hABFdisj : Disjoint (A ∩ B) F :=
   470	      (hFdisj.mono_right (Finset.inter_subset_left.trans Finset.subset_union_left)).symm
   118	lemma nrm_add_le_killed_add_killed {A B : Finset V} {p q : V} {M : Chain V}
   119	    (hkill : ∀ t ∈ M.support, Kkills A p t ∨ Kkills B q t)
   120	    {t₀ : Finset V} (ht₀ : t₀ ∈ M.support)
   121	    (hkA : Kkills A p t₀) (hkB : Kkills B q t₀) :
   122	    nrm M + (M t₀).natAbs ≤
   123	      nrm (M.filter (Kkills A p)) + nrm (M.filter (Kkills B q)) := by
   124	  classical
   125	  have hsubA : (M.filter (Kkills A p)).support ⊆ M.support := by
   126	    rw [Finsupp.support_filter]; exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
   127	  have hsubB : (M.filter (Kkills B q)).support ⊆ M.support := by
   128	    rw [Finsupp.support_filter]; exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
   129	  rw [nrm_eq_sum_subset hsubA, nrm_eq_sum_subset hsubB,
   130	    ← Finset.sum_add_distrib, nrm]
   131	  have hsplit : (M t₀).natAbs
   132	      = ∑ s ∈ M.support, if s = t₀ then (M t₀).natAbs else 0 := by
   133	    rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq' M.support t₀ fun _ => (M t₀).natAbs, if_pos ht₀]
   134	  rw [hsplit, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
   135	  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun s hs => ?_
   136	  rw [Finsupp.filter_apply, Finsupp.filter_apply]
   137	  by_cases hst : s = t₀
   138	  · subst hst
   139	    rw [if_pos hkA, if_pos hkB, if_pos rfl]
   140	  · rw [if_neg hst, add_zero]
   141	    rcases hkill s hs with h | h
   142	    · rw [if_pos h]
   143	      omega
   144	    · rw [if_pos h]
   145	      split <;> omega
   146	
   147	/-- No generator of a taut filling of `X + Y` is killed by both
   148	projections, for ANY admissible pair of base points. -/
   149	lemma no_double_kill {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ}
   150	    (hn : 1 ≤ n) {p q : V} (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   151	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   152	    {X Y : Chain V}
   153	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   154	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   155	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   156	    {M : Chain V} (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) (hMt : IsTaut M)
   157	    {p' q' : V} (hp' : p' ∈ A ∩ B) (hq' : q' ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq' : p' ≠ q')
   158	    {t₀ : Finset V} (ht₀ : t₀ ∈ M.support)
   159	    (hkA : Kkills A p' t₀) (hkB : Kkills B q' t₀) : False := by
   160	  have hbdsupp : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = n + 1 := by
   161	    intro s hs
   162	    rw [hM1] at hs
   163	    rcases Finset.mem_union.mp (Finsupp.support_add hs) with h | h
   164	    · exact (hX s h).2
   165	    · exact (hY s h).2
   166	  have hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2 :=
   167	    fun s hs => hMt.dim_pure hbdsupp s hs
   168	  have hdim2 : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card := by
   169	    intro s hs; rw [hpure s hs]; omega
   170	  have hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B := by
   171	    intro t ht
   172	    refine (supp_subset_vert ht).trans ((hMt.vert_subset hdim2).trans ?_)
   173	    rw [hM1]
   174	    exact (vert_add_subset X Y).trans (Finset.union_subset_union
   175	      (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hX s hs).1)
   176	      (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hY s hs).1))
   177	  have hbx : bdry (Kmap A p' M) = X := by
   178	    rw [bdry_Kmap A (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp').1, hM1, map_add,
   179	      Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (hX s hs).1),
   180	      Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn hp' hC hY hYc, add_zero]
   181	  have hby : bdry (Kmap B q' M) = Y := by
   182	    rw [bdry_Kmap B (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq').2, hM1, map_add,
   183	      Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (hY s hs).1),
   184	      Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hq')
   185	        (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hC) hX hXc, zero_add]
   186	  have h1 := nrm_Kmap_add_killed_le A p' M
   187	  have h2 := nrm_Kmap_add_killed_le B q' M
   188	  have h3 := nrm_add_le_killed_add_killed
   189	    (fun t ht => Kkills_or_Kkills hp' hq' hpq' hC (hsuppAB t ht) (hpure t ht))
   190	    ht₀ hkA hkB
   191	  have h4 : Zvol X ≤ nrm (Kmap A p' M) := Zvol_le hbx
   192	  have h5 : Zvol Y ≤ nrm (Kmap B q' M) := Zvol_le hby
   193	  have hadd := Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc
   194	  have hM2 : nrm M = Zvol (X + Y) := by rw [IsTaut, hM1] at hMt; exact hMt
   195	  have ht0ne : (M t₀).natAbs ≠ 0 :=
   196	    Int.natAbs_ne_zero.mpr (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mp ht₀)
   197	  omega
   198	
   199	/-! ## Step 2: generators are pure or extreme hybrids -/
   200	
   201	/-- With `n ≥ 2`, every generator of a taut filling of `X + Y` is pure
   202	(`⊆ A` or `⊆ B`) or an extreme hybrid: exactly one vertex on the
   203	opposite pure side and no shared vertices. -/
   204	lemma hybrid_structure {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ}
   205	    (hn : 2 ≤ n) {p q : V} (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   206	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   207	    {X Y : Chain V}
   208	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   209	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   210	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   211	    {M : Chain V} (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) (hMt : IsTaut M)
   212	    (hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2)
   213	    (hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B)
   214	    {t : Finset V} (ht : t ∈ M.support) :
   215	    t ⊆ A ∨ t ⊆ B ∨
   216	    ((t \ A).card = 1 ∧ t ∩ (A ∩ B) = ∅) ∨
   217	    ((t \ B).card = 1 ∧ t ∩ (A ∩ B) = ∅) := by
   218	  have hn1 : 1 ≤ n := by omega
   219	  have hpart := card_three_part (hsuppAB t ht)
   220	  rw [hpure t ht] at hpart
   221	  by_cases hxa : (t \ A).card = 0
   222	  · exact Or.inl (Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset.mp
   223	      (Finset.card_eq_zero.mp hxa))
   224	  by_cases hxb : (t \ B).card = 0
   225	  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset.mp
   226	      (Finset.card_eq_zero.mp hxb)))
   227	  by_cases hc0 : (t ∩ (A ∩ B)).card = 0
   228	  · by_cases hxa1 : (t \ A).card = 1
   229	    · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl ⟨hxa1, Finset.card_eq_zero.mp hc0⟩))
   230	    by_cases hxb1 : (t \ B).card = 1
   231	    · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨hxb1, Finset.card_eq_zero.mp hc0⟩))
   232	    exact (no_double_kill hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt hp hq hpq ht
   233	      (kills_of_two_sdiff p (by omega))
   234	      (kills_of_two_sdiff q (by omega))).elim
   235	  · have hcap : (t ∩ (A ∩ B)).Nonempty :=
   236	      Finset.card_pos.mp (by omega)
   237	    by_cases hxa1 : (t \ A).card = 1
   238	    · by_cases hxb1 : (t \ B).card = 1
   239	      · have hc2 : 2 ≤ (t ∩ (A ∩ B)).card := by omega
   240	        obtain ⟨p', hp', q', hq', hpq'⟩ := exists_pair_of_one_lt_card hc2
   241	        have hp'AB : p' ∈ A ∩ B := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp').2
   242	        have hq'AB : q' ∈ A ∩ B := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq').2
   243	        exact (no_double_kill hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt
   244	          hp'AB hq'AB hpq' ht
   245	          (kills_of_mem hxa1 (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp').1)
   246	          (kills_of_mem hxb1 (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq').1)).elim
   247	      · obtain ⟨p', hp'⟩ := hcap
   248	        have hp'AB : p' ∈ A ∩ B := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp').2
   249	        set q' := if p' = p then q else p with hq'def
   250	        have hq'AB : q' ∈ A ∩ B := by
   251	          rw [hq'def]; split <;> assumption
   252	        have hpq' : p' ≠ q' := by
   253	          rw [hq'def]
   254	          split
   255	          · rename_i h; rw [h]; exact hpq
   256	          · assumption
   257	        exact (no_double_kill hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt
   258	          hp'AB hq'AB hpq' ht
   259	          (kills_of_mem hxa1 (Finset.mem_inter.mp hp').1)
   260	          (kills_of_two_sdiff q' (by omega))).elim
   261	    · by_cases hxb1 : (t \ B).card = 1
   262	      · obtain ⟨q', hq'⟩ := hcap
   263	        have hq'AB : q' ∈ A ∩ B := (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq').2
   264	        set p' := if q' = p then q else p with hp'def
   265	        have hp'AB : p' ∈ A ∩ B := by
   266	          rw [hp'def]; split <;> assumption
   267	        have hpq' : p' ≠ q' := by
   268	          rw [hp'def]
   269	          split
   270	          · rename_i h; rw [h]; exact fun he => hpq he.symm
   271	          · rename_i h; exact fun he => h he.symm
   272	        exact (no_double_kill hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt
   273	          hp'AB hq'AB hpq' ht
   274	          (kills_of_two_sdiff p' (by omega))
   275	          (kills_of_mem hxb1 (Finset.mem_inter.mp hq').1)).elim
   276	      · exact (no_double_kill hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt hp hq hpq ht
   277	          (kills_of_two_sdiff p (by omega))
   278	          (kills_of_two_sdiff q (by omega))).elim
   279	
   280	/-! ## Step 3: extreme hybrids form a forbidden complete cone -/
   281	
   282	/-- No generator of a taut filling is an extreme hybrid on the `Y` side:
   283	the simplices `X^{n+1}y₀` would form a complete cone over `y₀`.  This is
   284	the coefficient-level vanishing argument: hybrid faces `X^n y₀` are
   285	absent from `X + Y`, and all their cofaces in `M` lie in the cone. -/
   286	lemma no_extreme_hybrid {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ}
   287	    (hn : 2 ≤ n) {p q : V} (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   288	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   289	    {X Y : Chain V}
   290	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   291	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   292	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   293	    {M : Chain V} (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) (hMt : IsTaut M)
   294	    (hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2)
   295	    (hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B)
   296	    {t₀ : Finset V} (ht₀ : t₀ ∈ M.support) {y₀ : V}
   297	    (hy : t₀ \ A = {y₀}) (hcap : t₀ ∩ (A ∩ B) = ∅) : False := by
   298	  classical
   299	  obtain ⟨hy₀t, hy₀A⟩ := mem_of_sdiff_singleton hy
   300	  -- the cone candidate: simplices with y₀ and at least two X-vertices
   301	  set U := M.filter (fun t => y₀ ∈ t ∧ 2 ≤ (t \ B).card) with hU
   302	  have hUsub : SubChain U M := subChain_filter _ M
   303	  have hUnbhd : nbhd y₀ U = U := by
   304	    ext t
   305	    rw [nbhd_apply]
   306	    split
   307	    · rfl
   308	    · rename_i hyt
   309	      rw [hU, Finsupp.filter_apply, if_neg (fun h => hyt h.1)]
   310	  have hUstruct : ∀ t ∈ U.support,
   311	      t \ A = {y₀} ∧ t ∩ (A ∩ B) = ∅ ∧ (t \ B).card = n + 1 := by
   312	    intro t ht
   313	    rw [hU, Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at ht
   314	    obtain ⟨htM, hyt, h2t⟩ := ht
   315	    rcases hybrid_structure hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt hpure
   316	      hsuppAB htM with h | h | ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2⟩
   317	    · exact absurd (h hyt) hy₀A
   318	    · rw [Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset.mpr h] at h2t
   319	      simp at h2t
   320	    · obtain ⟨y', hy'⟩ := Finset.card_eq_one.mp h1
   321	      have hyy' : y₀ ∈ t \ A := Finset.mem_sdiff.mpr ⟨hyt, hy₀A⟩
   322	      rw [hy', Finset.mem_singleton] at hyy'
   323	      subst hyy'
   324	      have hpart := card_three_part (hsuppAB t htM)
   325	      rw [hpure t htM, h2, hy'] at hpart
   326	      simp only [Finset.card_empty, Finset.card_singleton] at hpart
   327	      exact ⟨hy', h2, by omega⟩
   328	    · omega
   329	  have hxb₀ : (t₀ \ B).card = n + 1 := by
   330	    have hpart := card_three_part (hsuppAB t₀ ht₀)
   331	    rw [hpure t₀ ht₀, hcap, hy] at hpart
   332	    simp only [Finset.card_empty, Finset.card_singleton] at hpart
   333	    omega
   334	  have hUt₀ : U t₀ = M t₀ := by
   335	    rw [hU, Finsupp.filter_apply, if_pos ⟨hy₀t, by omega⟩]
   336	  have ht₀U : t₀ ∈ U.support := by
   337	    rw [Finsupp.mem_support_iff, hUt₀]
   338	    exact Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mp ht₀
   339	  have hbU : nbhd y₀ (bdry U) = 0 := by
   340	    ext s
   341	    rw [nbhd_apply, Finsupp.coe_zero, Pi.zero_apply]
   342	    split
   343	    · rename_i hys
   344	      by_cases hs2 : 2 ≤ (s \ B).card
   345	      · -- here ∂U agrees with ∂M, which carries no such face
   346	        have hMU : bdry (M - U) s = 0 := by
   347	          rw [bdry_apply_eq_sum]
   348	          refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun t ht => ?_
   349	          by_cases hgen : bdryGen t s = 0
   350	          · rw [hgen, mul_zero]
   351	          · obtain ⟨w, hw, hsw⟩ := exists_facet_of_bdryGen_ne_zero hgen
   352	            have hst : s ⊆ t := hsw ▸ Finset.erase_subset w t
   353	            have hzero : (M - U) t = 0 := by
   354	              rw [Finsupp.sub_apply, hU, Finsupp.filter_apply,
   355	                if_pos ⟨hst hys, le_trans hs2 (Finset.card_le_card
   356	                  (Finset.sdiff_subset_sdiff hst (Finset.Subset.refl B)))⟩,
   357	                sub_self]
   358	            rw [hzero, zero_mul]
   359	        have hXY0 : (X + Y) s = 0 := by
   360	          rw [Finsupp.add_apply]
   361	          have hXs : X s = 0 := by
   362	            by_contra h
   363	            exact hy₀A ((hX s (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mpr h)).1 hys)
   364	          have hYs : Y s = 0 := by
   365	            by_contra h
   366	            have hsB := (hY s (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mpr h)).1
   367	            rw [Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset.mpr hsB] at hs2
   368	            simp at hs2
   369	          rw [hXs, hYs, add_zero]
   370	        have hkey : bdry U s = bdry M s - bdry (M - U) s := by
   371	          rw [map_sub, Finsupp.sub_apply]
   372	          ring
   373	        rw [hkey, hMU, sub_zero, hM1, hXY0]
   374	      · -- faces of U-generators with y₀ have ≥ n ≥ 2 X-vertices
   375	        rw [bdry_apply_eq_sum]
   376	        refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun t ht => ?_
   377	        by_cases hgen : bdryGen t s = 0
   378	        · rw [hgen, mul_zero]
   379	        · obtain ⟨w, hw, hsw⟩ := exists_facet_of_bdryGen_ne_zero hgen
   380	          obtain ⟨htA, htcap, htxb⟩ := hUstruct t ht
   381	          have hcard : n ≤ (s \ B).card := by
   382	            rw [hsw, erase_sdiff]
   383	            have hle := Finset.pred_card_le_card_erase (a := w) (s := t \ B)
   384	            omega
   385	          exact absurd hcard (by omega)
   386	    · rfl
   387	  -- assemble the complete cone and contradict tautness
   388	  have hWclosed := bdry_lk_eq_zero hUnbhd hbU
   389	  have hdegy : deg y₀ (lk y₀ U) = 0 := by rw [deg, nbhd_lk, nrm_zero]
   390	  have hconeU : cone y₀ (lk y₀ U) = U := by rw [cone_lk, hUnbhd]
   391	  have hWne : lk y₀ U (t₀.erase y₀) ≠ 0 := by
   392	    intro h0
   393	    have happ : cone y₀ (lk y₀ U) t₀ = U t₀ := by rw [hconeU]
   394	    rw [cone_apply, if_pos hy₀t, h0, mul_zero] at happ
   395	    exact Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mp ht₀U happ.symm
   396	  have hne : (t₀.erase y₀).Nonempty := by
   397	    rw [← Finset.card_pos, Finset.card_erase_of_mem hy₀t, hpure t₀ ht₀]
   398	    omega
   399	  obtain ⟨x', hx'⟩ := hne
   400	  have hdegx' : deg x' (lk y₀ U) ≠ 0 := by
   401	    intro h
   402	    exact (deg_eq_zero_iff.mp h)
   403	      (mem_vert.mpr ⟨t₀.erase y₀, Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mpr hWne, hx'⟩)
   404	  exact not_taut_complete_cone hMt (by rw [hconeU]; exact hUsub)
   405	    hWclosed hdegy hdegx'

   407	/-! ## Theorem 1, part 2: taut fillings split -/
   408	
   409	/-- **Theorem 1, splitting** (Doyle–Ellison–Wang): for `n ≥ 2`, ANY taut
   410	filling `M` of an almost disjoint union `X + Y` splits: filtering `M`
   411	along `· ⊆ A` yields taut fillings of `X` and of `Y` summing to `M`. -/
   412	theorem IsTaut.splits {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ}
   413	    (hn : 2 ≤ n) {p q : V} (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   414	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   415	    {X Y : Chain V}
   416	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   417	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   418	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   419	    {M : Chain V} (hMt : IsTaut M) (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) :
   420	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) = X ∧
   421	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) = Y ∧
   422	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) ∧
   423	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) ∧
   424	    M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A) + M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A) = M := by
   425	  classical
   426	  have hn1 : 1 ≤ n := by omega
   427	  have hbdsupp : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = n + 1 := by
   428	    intro s hs
   429	    rw [hM1] at hs
   430	    rcases Finset.mem_union.mp (Finsupp.support_add hs) with h | h
   431	    · exact (hX s h).2
   432	    · exact (hY s h).2
   433	  have hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2 :=
   434	    fun s hs => hMt.dim_pure hbdsupp s hs
   435	  have hdim2 : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card := by
   436	    intro s hs; rw [hpure s hs]; omega
   437	  have hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B := by
   438	    intro t ht
   439	    refine (supp_subset_vert ht).trans ((hMt.vert_subset hdim2).trans ?_)
   440	    rw [hM1]
   441	    exact (vert_add_subset X Y).trans (Finset.union_subset_union
   442	      (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hX s hs).1)
   443	      (vert_subset_of_supp fun s hs => (hY s hs).1))
   444	  have hdich : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∨ t ⊆ B := by
   445	    intro t ht
   446	    rcases hybrid_structure hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt hpure
   447	      hsuppAB ht with h | h | ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2⟩
   448	    · exact Or.inl h
   449	    · exact Or.inr h
   450	    · obtain ⟨y₀, hy⟩ := Finset.card_eq_one.mp h1
   451	      exact (no_extreme_hybrid hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hM1 hMt hpure
   452	        hsuppAB ht hy h2).elim
   453	    · -- symmetric configuration, roles of A and B exchanged
   454	      obtain ⟨x₀, hx⟩ := Finset.card_eq_one.mp h1
   455	      have hM1' : bdry M = Y + X := by rw [hM1, add_comm]
   456	      have hsuppBA : ∀ t' ∈ M.support, t' ⊆ B ∪ A := by
   457	        intro t' ht'
   458	        rw [Finset.union_comm]
   459	        exact hsuppAB t' ht'
   460	      exact (no_extreme_hybrid hn (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hq)
   461	        (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hp) hpq.symm (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ hC)
   462	        hY hX hYc hXc hM1' hMt hpure hsuppBA ht hx
   463	        (Finset.inter_comm A B ▸ h2)).elim
   464	  set Mx := M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A) with hMx
   465	  set My := M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A) with hMy
   466	  have hsum : Mx + My = M := Finsupp.filter_pos_add_filter_neg M _
   467	  have hMxsupp : ∀ t ∈ Mx.support, t ⊆ A ∧ t.card = n + 2 := by
   468	    intro t ht
   469	    rw [hMx, Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at ht
   470	    exact ⟨ht.2, hpure t ht.1⟩
   471	  have hMysupp : ∀ t ∈ My.support, t ⊆ B ∧ t.card = n + 2 := by
   472	    intro t ht
   473	    rw [hMy, Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at ht
   474	    exact ⟨(hdich t ht.1).resolve_left ht.2, hpure t ht.1⟩
   475	  have hbsum : bdry Mx + bdry My = X + Y := by rw [← map_add, hsum, hM1]
   476	  have hbx : bdry Mx = X := by
   477	    have happly := congrArg (Kmap A p) hbsum
   478	    rw [map_add, map_add,
   479	      Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (bdry_supp_of_supp hMxsupp s hs).1),
   480	      Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn1 hp hC (bdry_supp_of_supp hMysupp)
   481	        (bdry_bdry My),
   482	      Kmap_eq_self (fun s hs => (hX s hs).1),
   483	      Kmap_eq_zero_of_closed hn1 hp hC hY hYc, add_zero, add_zero] at happly
   484	    exact happly
   485	  have hby : bdry My = Y := by
   486	    have h := hbsum
   487	    rw [hbx] at h
   488	    exact add_left_cancel h
   489	  have hnrm : nrm Mx + nrm My = nrm M := nrm_filter_add_nrm_filter_neg _ M
   490	  have hadd := Zvol_add_of_almost_disjoint hn1 hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc
   491	  have hM2 : nrm M = Zvol (X + Y) := by rw [IsTaut, hM1] at hMt; exact hMt
   492	  have h4 : Zvol X ≤ nrm Mx := Zvol_le hbx
   493	  have h5 : Zvol Y ≤ nrm My := Zvol_le hby
   494	  have hMxt : IsTaut Mx := by rw [IsTaut, hbx]; omega
   495	  have hMyt : IsTaut My := by rw [IsTaut, hby]; omega
   496	  exact ⟨hbx, hby, hMxt, hMyt, hsum⟩
   497	
   498	/-- **Theorem 1, splitting (full paper statement)**: for `n ≥ 2`, the
   499	hypothesis `(A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1` alone suffices — no witnessing pair
   500	`p ≠ q` is needed.  When `|A ∩ B| ≥ 2` we extract a pair and invoke
   501	`IsTaut.splits`; when `|A ∩ B| ≤ 1` we enlarge both sides by the same
   502	fresh vertices `F` to reach `|A' ∩ B'| = 2 ≤ n + 1`, run the split along
   503	`· ⊆ A'`, then transfer it back to `· ⊆ A`.  The transfer is valid because
   504	every generator of the filling `M` lies in `A ∪ B`, hence is disjoint from
   505	the fresh set `F`, so `t ⊆ A' ↔ t ⊆ A` on `M.support`.
   506	
   507	`[Infinite V]` only guarantees that fresh cut vertices are available for the
   508	`|A ∩ B| ≤ 1` enlargement; a taut filling never uses vertices beyond its boundary
   509	(`IsTaut.vert_subset`). -/
   510	theorem IsTaut.splits_full {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V] [Infinite V]
   511	    {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   512	    {X Y : Chain V}
   513	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   514	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   515	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   516	    {M : Chain V} (hMt : IsTaut M) (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) :
   517	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) = X ∧
   518	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) = Y ∧
   519	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) ∧
   520	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) ∧
   521	    M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A) + M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A) = M := by
   522	  classical
   523	  by_cases h2 : 2 ≤ (A ∩ B).card
   524	  · obtain ⟨p, hp, q, hq, hpq⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.mp h2
   525	    exact IsTaut.splits hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hMt hM1
   526	  · push_neg at h2
   527	    have hn1 : 1 ≤ n := by omega
   528	    have hle : (A ∪ B).card ≤ (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := Nat.le_add_right _ _
   529	    obtain ⟨W, hWsub, hWcard⟩ :=
   530	      Infinite.exists_superset_card_eq (A ∪ B) _ hle
   531	    set F := W \ (A ∪ B) with hF
   532	    have hFdisj : Disjoint F (A ∪ B) := Finset.sdiff_disjoint
   533	    have hFcard : F.card = 2 - (A ∩ B).card := by
   534	      have hkey : F.card + (A ∪ B).card = (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := by
   535	        rw [hF, Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card hWsub, hWcard]
   536	      omega
   537	    set A' := A ∪ F with hA'
   538	    set B' := B ∪ F with hB'
   539	    have hinter : A' ∩ B' = (A ∩ B) ∪ F := by
   540	      ext x
   541	      simp only [hA', hB', Finset.mem_inter, Finset.mem_union]
   542	      constructor
   543	      · rintro ⟨hxA | hxF, hxB | hxF⟩
   544	        · exact Or.inl ⟨hxA, hxB⟩
   545	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   546	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   547	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   548	      · rintro (⟨hxA, hxB⟩ | hxF)
   549	        · exact ⟨Or.inl hxA, Or.inl hxB⟩
   550	        · exact ⟨Or.inr hxF, Or.inr hxF⟩
   551	    have hABFdisj : Disjoint (A ∩ B) F :=
   552	      (hFdisj.mono_right (Finset.inter_subset_left.trans Finset.subset_union_left)).symm
   553	    have hcard' : (A' ∩ B').card = 2 := by
   554	      rw [hinter, Finset.card_union_of_disjoint hABFdisj, hFcard]
   555	      omega
   556	    have h1lt : 1 < (A' ∩ B').card := by rw [hcard']; omega
   557	    obtain ⟨p, hp, q, hq, hpq⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.mp h1lt
   558	    have hC' : (A' ∩ B').card ≤ n + 1 := by rw [hcard']; omega
   559	    have hAA' : A ⊆ A' := Finset.subset_union_left
   560	    have hBB' : B ⊆ B' := Finset.subset_union_left
   561	    have hX' : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A' ∧ s.card = n + 1 :=
   562	      fun s hs => ⟨(hX s hs).1.trans hAA', (hX s hs).2⟩
   563	    have hY' : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B' ∧ s.card = n + 1 :=
   564	      fun s hs => ⟨(hY s hs).1.trans hBB', (hY s hs).2⟩
   565	    obtain ⟨hbx', hby', hMxt', hMyt', hsum'⟩ :=
   566	      IsTaut.splits hn hp hq hpq hC' hX' hY' hXc hYc hMt hM1
   567	    have hbdsupp : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = n + 1 := by
   568	      intro s hs
   569	      rw [hM1] at hs
   570	      rcases Finset.mem_union.mp (Finsupp.support_add hs) with h | h
   571	      · exact (hX s h).2
   572	      · exact (hY s h).2
   573	    have hpure : ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = n + 2 :=
   574	      fun s hs => hMt.dim_pure hbdsupp s hs
   575	    have hdim2 : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card := by
   576	      intro s hs; rw [hpure s hs]; omega
   577	    have hsuppAB : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t ⊆ A ∪ B := by
   578	      intro t ht
   579	      refine (supp_subset_vert ht).trans ((hMt.vert_subset hdim2).trans ?_)
   580	      rw [hM1]

   545	  have hr2 : finrank (ZMod 2) (LinearMap.range (bd2 σ)) = σ.card - 1 := by

   547	    rw [finrank_ker_bd2 h.toClosedSurface h.nonempty, hF] at this; omega
   548	  have hk1 : finrank (ZMod 2) (LinearMap.ker (bd1 σ))
   549	      = (edgesOf σ).card - ((vertsOf σ).card - 1) := by
   550	    have := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker (bd1 σ)
   551	    rw [finrank_range_bd1 h.toClosedSurface h.vertsOf_nonempty, hE] at this; omega
   552	  have he := h.euler
   553	  have hV : 1 ≤ (vertsOf σ).card := h.vertsOf_nonempty.card_pos
   554	  rw [hr2, hk1]; omega
   555	
   556	/-! ### χ ≤ 2 for closed surfaces (no Euler hypothesis) -/
   557	
   558	/-- **χ ≤ 2** for any combinatorial closed surface. From `b₂ = 1`, `r₁ = V−1`,
   559	and `range ∂₂ ≤ ker ∂₁` (∂∂ = 0): `dim range ∂₂ ≤ dim ker ∂₁`, i.e.
   560	`(F − 1) ≤ (E − (V − 1))`, which is `V + F ≤ E + 2`. (Equality is the watershed,
   561	which needs the extra Euler input.) -/
   562	theorem chi_le_two {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsClosedSurface σ)
   563	    (hne0 : (vertsOf σ).Nonempty) :
   564	    (vertsOf σ).card + σ.card ≤ (edgesOf σ).card + 2 := by
   565	  have hσne : σ.Nonempty := by
    45	    Finset.sum_boole, card_edges_through_vertex hγe hγ3 x]
    46	  split <;> decide
    47	
    48	/-- **The cut exists.** A non-face triangle's edge-cycle bounds a face-set `W`
    49	(this is exactly where H₁ = 0 is spent). -/
    50	theorem exists_cut {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsSphere2 σ) {γ : Finset V}
    51	    (hγ3 : γ.card = 3) (hγe : γ.powersetCard 2 ⊆ edgesOf σ) :
    52	    ∃ W : C2 σ, bd2 σ W = gammaChain σ γ := by
    53	  have hcyc : gammaChain σ γ ∈ LinearMap.ker (bd1 σ) :=
    54	    LinearMap.mem_ker.mpr (bd1_gammaChain hγ3 hγe)
    55	  rw [← range_bd2_eq_ker_bd1 h] at hcyc
    56	  exact LinearMap.mem_range.mp hcyc
    57	
    58	/-! ## The two sides -/
    59	
    60	/-- One side of the cut: the faces on which the bounding chain `W` is `1`. -/
    61	noncomputable def cutSet (σ : Finset (Finset V)) (W : C2 σ) : Finset (Finset V) :=
   300	  have hz : bdry (X.filter (fun s => ¬ P s)) u = 0 := by
   301	    rw [bdry_apply_eq_sum]
   302	    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun t ht => ?_
   303	    rw [Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at ht
   304	    rw [hvanish t ht.1 ht.2, mul_zero]
   305	  have hsum : bdry (X.filter P) u + bdry (X.filter (fun s => ¬ P s)) u = bdry X u := by

   309	/-- **The edge-join closed-filter lemma.** If `X` is a closed chain whose faces
   310	are triangles each lying on the `A`-side or the `B`-side, and `A ∩ B` is a single
   311	edge, then the `A`-side filter of `X` is again closed — its boundary is a closed

   315	lemma bdry_filter_subset_eq_zero_of_inter_card_two {X : Chain V} {A B : Finset V}

   317	    (hAB2 : (A ∩ B).card = 2) :

   328	  have huB : u ⊆ B := by

     1	import Taut.Ball

     3	
     4	/-!
     5	# Pseudomanifold invariant — the "simplicial" half of a sticker ball
     6	
     7	`FreelyShellable`/`IsBall` originally tracked only the shelling (boundary by
     8	symmetric difference), which admits non-simplicial configurations: a triangle
     9	can be re-exposed into a third tet (an edge "flipped back and forth").  A sticker
    10	ball *for us* is a shellable **simplicial** complex, so we carry the
    11	pseudomanifold condition — every triangle lies in at most two tets — as part of
    12	the invariant.  This file sets up the face-incidence bookkeeping.
    13	-/
    14	
    15	namespace Taut
    16	
    17	open Finset
    18	
    19	variable {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V]
    20	
    21	/-- Number of tets of `τ` that contain the face `f`. -/
    22	def faceCount (τ : Finset (Finset V)) (f : Finset V) : ℕ :=
    23	  (τ.filter (fun t => f ⊆ t)).card
    24	
    25	/-- `τ` is a 3-pseudomanifold: every triangle lies in at most two tets.
    26	With a unit boundary this forces every boundary triangle into exactly one tet
    27	(two would cancel to `0` or sum to `±2`, never the boundary value `±1`). -/
    28	def IsPseudomanifold (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    29	  ∀ f, f.card = 3 → faceCount τ f ≤ 2
    30	
    31	/-- `TriangleBounded3` — the triangle-count condition (`IsPseudomanifold`): every
    32	triangle lies in at most two tets.  This is *not* a normal/clean pseudomanifold:
    33	it admits two balls joined along an edge, or along a single vertex. -/
    34	abbrev TriangleBounded3 (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop := IsPseudomanifold τ
    35	
    36	/-- A face contained in no tet has incidence zero — used to discharge the
    37	triangle-cleanliness hypothesis from the triangle rule-out (`s₁,s₂ ∉ M−t`). -/
    38	lemma faceCount_eq_zero {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {f : Finset V}
    39	    (h : ∀ t ∈ τ, ¬ f ⊆ t) : faceCount τ f = 0 := by
    40	  unfold faceCount
    41	  rw [Finset.filter_false_of_mem h]; rfl
    42	
    43	lemma faceCount_insert_of_not_mem {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {t : Finset V}
    44	    (ht : t ∉ τ) (f : Finset V) :
    45	    faceCount (insert t τ) f = faceCount τ f + (if f ⊆ t then 1 else 0) := by
    46	  unfold faceCount
    47	  rw [Finset.filter_insert]
    48	  by_cases h : f ⊆ t
    49	  · have hnm : t ∉ τ.filter (fun s => f ⊆ s) :=
    50	      fun hmem => ht (Finset.mem_of_mem_filter t hmem)
    51	    simp only [if_pos h, Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hnm]
    52	  · simp only [if_neg h, Nat.add_zero]
    53	
    54	lemma isPseudomanifold_singleton (t : Finset V) :
    55	    IsPseudomanifold ({t} : Finset (Finset V)) := by
    56	  intro f _
    57	  have h : faceCount ({t} : Finset (Finset V)) f ≤ 1 := by
    58	    unfold faceCount
    59	    have hle := Finset.card_filter_le ({t} : Finset (Finset V)) (fun s => f ⊆ s)
    60	    simpa using hle
    61	  omega
    62	
    63	/-- **Clean glue preserves the pseudomanifold property.** Inserting a fresh tet
    64	`t` whose every *triangle* lies in at most one existing tet keeps `τ` a
    65	pseudomanifold.  The hypothesis `hclean` is exactly the "no re-exposure"
    66	condition the forward construction must supply at each shelling step. -/
    67	lemma isPseudomanifold_insert {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {t : Finset V}
    68	    (hτ : IsPseudomanifold τ) (ht : t ∉ τ)
    69	    (hclean : ∀ f, f.card = 3 → f ⊆ t → faceCount τ f ≤ 1) :
    70	    IsPseudomanifold (insert t τ) := by
    71	  intro f hf
    72	  rw [faceCount_insert_of_not_mem ht]
    73	  by_cases h : f ⊆ t
    74	  · rw [if_pos h]; have := hclean f hf h; omega
    75	  · rw [if_neg h]; have := hτ f hf; omega
    76	
    77	/-! ## Edge-link connectedness — the manifold-along-edges half of a stickerball
    78	
    79	`IsPseudomanifold` controls triangle incidence, but a boundary-tracking shelling
    80	cannot see when an edge acquires a disconnected link (a stray ring of tets).  A
    81	genuine simplicial ball has connected edge-links, so a faithful stickerball must
    82	carry that too. -/
    83	
    84	/-- The link of an edge `e` in a tet-set `τ`, as a simple graph on apexes:
    85	`x ~ y` iff `e ∪ {x, y}` is a tet of `τ`. -/
    86	def edgeLinkGraph (τ : Finset (Finset V)) (e : Finset V) : SimpleGraph V where
    87	  Adj x y := x ≠ y ∧ insert x (insert y e) ∈ τ
    88	  symm := by
    89	    intro x y h
    90	    exact ⟨h.1.symm, by rw [Finset.insert_comm]; exact h.2⟩
    91	  loopless := ⟨fun _ h => h.1 rfl⟩
    92	
    93	/-- The apexes of edge `e` in `τ`: the vertices of the tets containing `e`, with
    94	`e` itself removed. -/
    95	def edgeLinkVerts (τ : Finset (Finset V)) (e : Finset V) : Finset V :=
    96	  vertsOf (τ.filter (fun t => e ⊆ t)) \ e
    97	
    98	/-- `τ` is *edge-link connected*: for every edge, its apexes are mutually
    99	reachable in the edge-link graph (one connected ring, no stray components). -/
   100	def EdgeLinkConnected (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
   101	  ∀ e : Finset V, e.card = 2 → ConnOn (edgeLinkGraph τ e) (edgeLinkVerts τ e)
   102	
   103	/-- An edge contained in no tet has empty apex set — used to discharge the
   104	edge-cleanliness hypothesis from the edge rule-out (`ab ∉ M−t`). -/
   105	lemma edgeLinkVerts_eq_empty {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {e : Finset V}
   106	    (h : ∀ t ∈ τ, ¬ e ⊆ t) : edgeLinkVerts τ e = ∅ := by
   107	  unfold edgeLinkVerts vertsOf
   108	  rw [Finset.filter_false_of_mem h]; simp
   109	
   110	lemma edgeLinkGraph_mono {τ τ' : Finset (Finset V)} (h : τ ⊆ τ') (e : Finset V) :
   111	    edgeLinkGraph τ e ≤ edgeLinkGraph τ' e :=
   112	  fun _ _ hxy => ⟨hxy.1, h hxy.2⟩
   113	
   114	/-- Reachability in a smaller tet-set's edge-link transfers to the larger one;
   115	contrapositively, **deleting tets cannot reconnect a split edge-link** — the
   116	key fact behind the edge rule-out. -/
   117	lemma edgeLink_reachable_mono {τ τ' : Finset (Finset V)} (h : τ ⊆ τ') (e : Finset V)
   118	    {x y : V} (hr : (edgeLinkGraph τ e).Reachable x y) :
   119	    (edgeLinkGraph τ' e).Reachable x y :=
   120	  hr.mono (edgeLinkGraph_mono h e)
   121	
   122	lemma edgeLinkConnected_singleton {t : Finset V} (ht : t.card = 4) :
   123	    EdgeLinkConnected ({t} : Finset (Finset V)) := by
   124	  intro e he x hx y hy
   125	  simp only [edgeLinkVerts, Finset.mem_sdiff, mem_vertsOf, Finset.mem_filter,
   126	    Finset.mem_singleton] at hx hy
   127	  obtain ⟨⟨_, ⟨rfl, hesub⟩, hxt⟩, hxe⟩ := hx
   128	  obtain ⟨⟨_, ⟨rfl, _⟩, hyt⟩, hye⟩ := hy
   129	  by_cases hxy : x = y
   130	  · subst hxy; exact ⟨SimpleGraph.Walk.nil⟩
   131	  · refine SimpleGraph.Adj.reachable ⟨hxy, ?_⟩
   132	    rw [Finset.mem_singleton]
   133	    have hxni : x ∉ insert y e := by
   134	      simp only [Finset.mem_insert, not_or]; exact ⟨hxy, hxe⟩
   135	    have hcard : (insert x (insert y e)).card = 4 := by
   136	      rw [Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hxni, Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hye]
   137	      omega
   138	    refine Finset.eq_of_subset_of_card_le (fun z hz => ?_) (le_of_eq (ht.trans hcard.symm))
   139	    simp only [Finset.mem_insert] at hz
   140	    rcases hz with rfl | rfl | hz
   141	    · exact hxt
   142	    · exact hyt
   143	    · exact hesub hz
   144	
   145	lemma insert_apexes_eq {t e : Finset V} {z w : V} (ht : t.card = 4) (he : e.card = 2)
   146	    (hsub : e ⊆ t) (hz : z ∈ t \ e) (hw : w ∈ t \ e) (hzw : z ≠ w) :
   147	    insert z (insert w e) = t := by
   148	  rw [Finset.mem_sdiff] at hz hw
   149	  have hwni : w ∉ e := hw.2
   150	  have hzni : z ∉ insert w e := by
   151	    simp only [Finset.mem_insert, not_or]; exact ⟨hzw, hz.2⟩
   152	  have hcard : (insert z (insert w e)).card = 4 := by
   153	    rw [Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hzni, Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hwni]; omega
   154	  refine Finset.eq_of_subset_of_card_le (fun u hu => ?_) (le_of_eq (ht.trans hcard.symm))
   155	  simp only [Finset.mem_insert] at hu
   156	  rcases hu with rfl | rfl | hu
   157	  · exact hz.1
   158	  · exact hw.1
   159	  · exact hsub hu

   161	lemma edgeLinkVerts_insert_of_subset {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {t e : Finset V}

   187	/-- **Edge-link connectedness is preserved by inserting a fresh tet**, provided

   191	lemma edgeLinkConnected_insert {τ : Finset (Finset V)} {t : Finset V} (ht : t.card = 4)
   192	    (hτ : EdgeLinkConnected τ)
   193	    (hcompat : ∀ e, e ⊆ t → e.card = 2 →

  1530	/-- **PM-carry: every taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a
  1531	pseudomanifold.** A standalone strong induction
  1532	carrying `IsPseudomanifold M.support` (each triangle in ≤ 2 tets). Base case
  1533	(≤ 4 vertices) by `base_isPM`; degree-3 vertex by `deg3_isPM`; otherwise by
  1534	`prime_isPM`; the strong induction hypothesis is threaded to both non-base
  1535	steps. -/
  1536	theorem taut_isPseudomanifold {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  1537	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  1538	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsPseudomanifold M.support := by
  1539	  suffices H : ∀ N, ∀ (σ : Finset (Finset V)) (X M : Chain V), nrm M = N → IsSphere2 σ →
  1540	      UnitOn X σ → bdry X = 0 → bdry M = X → IsTaut M → SimplicialChain M →
  1541	      IsPseudomanifold M.support by
  1542	    exact H (nrm M) σ X M rfl hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS
  1543	  intro N
  1544	  induction N using Nat.strong_induction_on with
  1545	  | _ N IH =>
  1546	    intro σ X M hN hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS
  1547	    by_cases hv : (vertsOf σ).card ≤ 4
  1548	    · exact base_isPM σ X M hσ hU hMX hT hS hv
  1549	    · push_neg at hv
  1550	      by_cases hd3 : HasDegree3Vertex σ
  1551	      · refine deg3_isPM σ X M hσ hv hU hXc hMX hT hS hd3 ?_
  1552	        intro σ' X' M' hlt hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
  1553	        exact IH (nrm M') (hN ▸ hlt) σ' X' M' rfl hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
  1554	      · have hno3 : NoDegree3Vertex σ := fun v hvv hcard => hd3 ⟨v, hvv, hcard⟩
  1555	        refine prime_isPM σ X M hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS hno3 ?_
