    13	      4 < (vertsOf σ).card → UnitOn X σ → bdry X = 0 → bdry M = X → IsTaut M →
    14	      SimplicialChain M → HasDegree3Vertex σ →
    15	      (∀ (σ' : Finset (Finset V)) (X' M' : Chain V), nrm M' < nrm M → IsSphere2 σ' →
    16	        UnitOn X' σ' → bdry X' = 0 → bdry M' = X' → IsTaut M' → SimplicialChain M' →
    17	        FreelyCleanShellable M'.support σ') →
    18	      FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ)
    19	    (prime_step_clean : ∀ (σ : Finset (Finset V)) (X M : Chain V), IsSphere2 σ →
    20	      4 < (vertsOf σ).card → UnitOn X σ → bdry X = 0 → bdry M = X → IsTaut M →
    21	      SimplicialChain M → NoDegree3Vertex σ →
    22	      (∀ (σ' : Finset (Finset V)) (X' M' : Chain V), nrm M' < nrm M → IsSphere2 σ' →
    23	        UnitOn X' σ' → bdry X' = 0 → bdry M' = X' → IsTaut M' → SimplicialChain M' →
    24	        FreelyCleanShellable M'.support σ') →
    25	      FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ)
    26	    {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ) (hU : UnitOn X σ)
    27	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M) (hS : SimplicialChain M) :
    28	    FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ := by
    29	  suffices H : ∀ N, ∀ (σ : Finset (Finset V)) (X M : Chain V), nrm M = N → IsSphere2 σ →
    30	      UnitOn X σ → bdry X = 0 → bdry M = X → IsTaut M → SimplicialChain M →
    31	      FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ by
    32	    exact H (nrm M) σ X M rfl hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS
    33	  intro N
    34	  induction N using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    35	  | _ N IH =>
    36	    intro σ X M hN hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS
    37	    by_cases hv : (vertsOf σ).card ≤ 4
    38	    · exact base_clean σ X M hσ hU hMX hT hS hv
    39	    · push_neg at hv
    40	      by_cases hd3 : HasDegree3Vertex σ
    41	      · refine deg3_step_clean σ X M hσ hv hU hXc hMX hT hS hd3 ?_
    42	        intro σ' X' M' hlt hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
    43	        exact IH (nrm M') (hN ▸ hlt) σ' X' M' rfl hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
    44	      · have hno3 : NoDegree3Vertex σ := fun v hvv hcard => hd3 ⟨v, hvv, hcard⟩
    45	        refine prime_step_clean σ X M hσ hv hU hXc hMX hT hS hno3 ?_
    46	        intro σ' X' M' hlt hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
    47	        exact IH (nrm M') (hN ▸ hlt) σ' X' M' rfl hσ' hU' hX'c hM'X' hT' hS'
    48	
    49	/-- **base_free_clean** (the discharged base hole of `theorem3_core_clean`): a taut
    50	filling of a 2-sphere on ≤ 4 vertices is a *free clean* sticker ball — it is a single
    51	tetrahedron (`freelyCleanShellable_singleton`). -/
    52	theorem base_free_clean (σ : Finset (Finset V)) (X M : Chain V) (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
    53	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M) (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    54	    (hv : (vertsOf σ).card ≤ 4) : FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ := by

    60	    aleph_base_support_eq_singleton_of_four_vertices hcard hsuppInfo hne
  3379	theorem theorem3_clean {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3380	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)

  3383	
  3384	/-- **Theorem 2 (clean route)**: a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere `σ` is a *clean ball*
  3385	(`IsCleanBall` = the paper's *stickerball* = a shellable clean ball: it admits a clean shelling).
  3386	
  3387	Two facts about this endpoint are worth making explicit (both are *named theorems*, not handwaves):
  3388	
  3389	* **It is genuinely clean, including connected vertex links.**  `IsCleanBall M.support σ` provably
  3390	  entails `Clean3Complex M.support` via `IsCleanBall.clean3Complex` — exported here as the standalone
  3391	  endpoint `taut_clean3Complex`.  `Clean3Complex = Pure3 ∧ TriangleBounded3 ∧ Normal3` and
  3392	  `Normal3 = EdgeLinkConnected ∧ VertexLinkConnected`, so connected vertex links (dim-0 links) and
  3393	  connected edge links (dim-1 links) — the paper's "normal" condition — are both delivered.  The
  3394	  per-step vertex-link compatibility is the `CleanGlueStep.hvlc` field, folded by
  3395	  `IsCleanShelling.clean3Complex`.
  3396	
  3397	* **The proof actually establishes the stronger free-start statement.**  `IsCleanBall` asks only for
  3398	  *one* clean shelling, but this theorem is a one-line weakening of `theorem3_clean`, which produces a
  3399	  clean shelling beginning with *any* prescribed tet (`FreelyCleanShellable`).  So the substance of
  3400	  Theorem 3 (free choice of starting tet) is already proved by the same minimal-counterexample
  3401	  induction; `theorem2_clean` simply forgets the head condition.  `IsCleanBall` (stickerball /
  3402	  shellable) and `FreelyCleanShellable` (free-start) are kept as *distinct* predicates on purpose —
  3403	  "stickerball" is not silently redefined to mean "freely shellable". -/
  3404	theorem theorem2_clean {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3405	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3406	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsCleanBall M.support σ := by
  3407	  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := aleph_base_support_nonempty hσ hU hMX
  3408	  obtain ⟨l, _, hlτ, hlnodup, hlshell⟩ := theorem3_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS t ht
  3409	  exact ⟨l, hlτ, hlnodup, hlshell⟩
  3410	
  3411	/-- **Theorem 2, normality endpoint**: a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *clean
  3412	3-complex* — `Pure3 ∧ TriangleBounded3 ∧ Normal3`, where `Normal3 = EdgeLinkConnected ∧
  3413	VertexLinkConnected`.  This makes the paper's "normal" condition (connected vertex *and* edge links)
  3414	a first-class named conclusion rather than something a consumer must unpack from `IsCleanBall`.
  3415	In particular `(taut_clean3Complex …).2.2.2 : VertexLinkConnected M.support`. -/
  3416	theorem taut_clean3Complex {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3417	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3418	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : Clean3Complex M.support :=
  3419	  (theorem2_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS).clean3Complex
  3420	
  3421	/-! ## Public endpoints (mathematically named)
  3422	
  3423	The primary, paper-independent statements.  A taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is an
  3424	*anyrooted stickerball*; the weaker certificates are corollaries. -/
  3425	
  3426	/-- **Main theorem (Theorems 2+3 unified): a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is an anyrooted
  3427	stickerball.**  That is, its support is a shellable clean 3-complex with nonempty boundary
  3428	(`IsStickerball` — the combinatorial `B³` certificate) *and* a clean shelling can begin with any
  3429	prescribed tetrahedron (`FreelyCleanShellable`).  Subsumes both the ordinary stickerball certificate
  3430	(paper Theorem 2) and the free choice of starting tet (paper Theorem 3): the minimal-counterexample
  3431	induction proves the anyrooted statement directly (`theorem3_clean`), of which the existence form
  3432	(`theorem2_clean`) is the weakening. -/

  3435	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsAnyrootedStickerball M.support σ :=
  3436	  ⟨⟨theorem2_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS, hσ.nonempty⟩, theorem3_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS⟩
  3437

  3445	/-- A taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *clean 3-complex* — pure, every triangle in one or

   398	`TriangleBounded3 ∧ EdgeLinkConnected` is still not the right invariant — it omits
   399	vertex links (two tets sharing one vertex pass both yet pinch).  The honest
   400	condition is `Clean3Complex`: pure, triangle-bounded, and *normal* (both links
   401	connected).  This is what the public shellability predicate must certify. -/
   402	
   403	/-- `Pure3 τ`: every tet has 4 vertices (genuine 3-dimensionality). -/
   404	def Pure3 (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop := ∀ t ∈ τ, t.card = 4
   405	
   406	/-- `Normal3 τ`: connected edge links *and* connected vertex links — the normal
   407	(no-pinch) condition.  `TriangleBounded3 ∧ EdgeLinkConnected` alone is not normal. -/
   408	def Normal3 (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
   409	  EdgeLinkConnected τ ∧ VertexLinkConnected τ
   410	
   411	/-- `Clean3Complex τ`: pure, triangle-bounded, and normal — the honest
   412	"simplicial triangulation of a 3-ball" invariant the public shelling must carry,
   413	not the boundary-only trace.  Exposed under the `Is`-prefixed names
   414	`IsClean3Complex` / `IsNormal3Psman` as the preferred public vocabulary. -/
   415	def Clean3Complex (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
   416	  Pure3 τ ∧ TriangleBounded3 τ ∧ Normal3 τ
   417	
   418	/-- **`IsClean3Complex`** — the clean (normal) 3-complex condition: pure, every triangle in one or two
   419	tets, and *both* links connected (`VertexLinkConnected` and `EdgeLinkConnected`).  This is the local
   420	combinatorial-manifold condition; preferred public name for `Clean3Complex`. -/
   421	abbrev IsClean3Complex (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop := Clean3Complex τ
   422	
   423	/-- **`IsNormal3Psman`** — synonym for `IsClean3Complex`: a normal 3-pseudomanifold (triangle-bounded
   424	with both links connected, pure). -/
   425	abbrev IsNormal3Psman (τ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop := Clean3Complex τ

    74	/-- The filling volume: the least L¹ norm of a chain with boundary `X`. -/
    75	noncomputable def Zvol (X : Chain V) : ℕ :=
    76	  sInf {n | ∃ M : Chain V, bdry M = X ∧ nrm M = n}
    77	
    78	/-- `M` is an optimal filling of its own boundary. -/
    79	def IsTaut (M : Chain V) : Prop := nrm M = Zvol (bdry M)
    80	
    81	lemma Zvol_le {X M : Chain V} (h : bdry M = X) : Zvol X ≤ nrm M :=
    82	  Nat.sInf_le ⟨M, h, rfl⟩

    18	/-- A chain `X` is a *unit chain on `σ`* if it is supported exactly on `σ` with
    19	every coefficient `±1`. The boundary `X(σ)` of a triangulated sphere is such a
    20	chain (some choice of orientation); nothing downstream depends on the choice. -/
    21	def UnitOn (X : Chain V) (σ : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    22	  X.support = σ ∧ ∀ s ∈ σ, X s = 1 ∨ X s = -1
    23	
    24	/-- A chain is *simplicial* if every coefficient is `-1`, `0`, or `1` — i.e. it
    25	is a genuine multiset of oriented simplices with no repeats. A taut filling that
    26	"arises from a triangulation" is such a chain on the tets of a ball. -/
    27	def SimplicialChain (M : Chain V) : Prop :=
    28	  ∀ t, M t = -1 ∨ M t = 0 ∨ M t = 1

   113	theorem Zvol_add_deg_le (x : V) {X : Chain V} (hX : bdry X = 0) :
   114	    Zvol X + deg x X ≤ nrm X := by
   115	  have h1 : Zvol X ≤ nrm (cone x X) := Zvol_le (bdry_cone_of_closed hX)
   116	  have h2 := nrm_cone_add_deg x X
   117	  omega
   118	
   119	/-- Proposition 3 (nocone): a taut chain contains no nontrivial complete
   120	cone.  Here `cone x W ⊂ M` with `W` closed, `x ∉ vert W` (i.e.
   121	`deg x W = 0`), and nontriviality is witnessed by a vertex `x'` of `W`. -/
   122	theorem not_taut_complete_cone {x x' : V} {W M : Chain V}
   123	    (hM : IsTaut M) (hU : SubChain (cone x W) M) (hW : bdry W = 0)
   124	    (hx : deg x W = 0) (hx' : deg x' W ≠ 0) : False := by
   125	  have hUt : IsTaut (cone x W) := hM.subChain hU
   126	  have hbd : bdry (cone x W) = W := bdry_cone_of_closed hW
   127	  have h1 := nrm_cone_add_deg x W
   128	  have h2 := Zvol_add_deg_le x' hW
   129	  rw [IsTaut, hbd] at hUt
   130	  omega
   131	
   132	/-- Proposition 4 (nointernal): a taut chain all of whose simplices have
   133	at least two vertices has no internal vertices. -/
   134	theorem IsTaut.no_internal_vertex {M : Chain V} (hM : IsTaut M) {x : V}
   135	    (hdim : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card)
   136	    (hxM : x ∈ vert M) (hxB : x ∉ vert (bdry M)) : False := by
   137	  set W := lk x M with hWdef
   138	  have hUc : cone x W = nbhd x M := cone_lk x M
   139	  have hx : deg x W = 0 := by rw [deg, hWdef, nbhd_lk, nrm_zero]
   140	  have hWfree : nbhd x W = 0 := nbhd_lk x M
   141	  have hWb : nbhd x (bdry W) = 0 := nbhd_bdry_eq_zero hWfree
   142	  have hcb : cone x (bdry W) = 0 := by
   143	    have hhom := bdry_cone_add_cone_bdry x W
   144	    have h0 : nbhd x (bdry (cone x W)) = 0 := by
   145	      rw [hUc, nbhd_bdry_nbhd, nbhd_eq_zero_iff.mpr hxB]
   139	theorem IsTaut.dim_pure {M : Chain V} (hM : IsTaut M) {k : ℕ}
   140	    (hbd : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = k) :
   141	    ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = k + 1 := by
   142	  set P := dimPart (k + 1) M with hP
   143	  have hPM : SubChain P M := subChain_filter _ M
   144	  have hbdP : bdry P = bdry M := by
   145	    rw [hP, ← dimPart_bdry, dimPart_eq_self hbd]
   146	  have h1 : Zvol (bdry M) ≤ nrm P := Zvol_le hbdP
   147	  have h2 := hPM.nrm_add_nrm_sub
   148	  rw [IsTaut] at hM
   149	  have h0 : nrm (M - P) = 0 := by omega
   150	  have hMP : M = P := sub_eq_zero.mp (nrm_eq_zero_iff.mp h0)
   151	  intro s hs
   152	  rw [hMP, hP] at hs
   153	  rw [dimPart, Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at hs
   154	  exact hs.2
   155	
   156	/-- A taut chain in dimensions ≥ 1 has all its vertices on its boundary. -/
   157	theorem IsTaut.vert_subset {M : Chain V} (hM : IsTaut M)
   158	    (hdim : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card) :
   159	    vert M ⊆ vert (bdry M) := by
   160	  intro x hx
   161	  by_contra hxB
   162	  exact hM.no_internal_vertex hdim hx hxB
   163	
   164	/-! ## K on supported chains, and recovery -/
   345	theorem nrm_Kmap_add_killed_le (A : Finset V) (p : V) (M : Chain V) :
   346	    nrm (Kmap A p M) + nrm (M.filter (Kkills A p)) ≤ nrm M := by
   347	  classical
   348	  have h1 : nrm (Kmap A p M) ≤
   349	      ∑ s ∈ M.support.filter (fun s => ¬ Kkills A p s), (M s).natAbs := by
   350	    calc nrm (Kmap A p M) ≤ ∑ s ∈ M.support, nrm (M s • KGen A p s) := by
   351	          rw [Kmap_eq_sum]; exact nrm_sum_le _ _
   352	      _ = ∑ s ∈ M.support, (M s).natAbs * nrm (KGen A p s) := by

Build check: `lake build` succeeds. Axiom check for the status-table endpoints reports only `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`. No `sorry`, `admit`, project `axiom`, `unsafe`, or `native_decide` found under `lean/Taut`.

**Discrepancies Found**

1. [notes/taut-lean-proof.tex](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:72)
Claim: “a taut chain contains no complete cone `y * L` over a cycle `L`.”
Lean: [Zvol.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Zvol.lean:122) `not_taut_complete_cone` requires `SubChain (cone x W) M`, `bdry W = 0`, `deg x W = 0`, and nontriviality `deg x' W ≠ 0`.
Verdict: MISSING-HYPOTHESIS / OVERCLAIM.
Correction: “A taut chain contains no nontrivial complete cone `cone x W` as a subchain, where `W` is closed, the apex is absent from `W`, and `W` has a nonzero vertex degree.”

2. [notes/taut-lean-proof.tex](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:173)
Claim: “supported on a set of at most `n+1` vertices is zero (`eq_zero_of_closed_supp_card_eq`).”
Lean: [Projection.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Projection.lean:394) `eq_zero_of_closed_supp_card_eq` is the equality case `W.card = k`; the strict case is [Projection.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Projection.lean:383) `eq_zero_of_supp_card_lt`.
Verdict: WRONG-CITATION.
Correction: “If the support vertices are fewer than `n+1`, use `eq_zero_of_supp_card_lt`; if exactly `n+1` and closed, use `eq_zero_of_closed_supp_card_eq`.”

3. [notes/taut-lean-proof.tex](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:198)
Claim: “Concatenating minimal fillings … with no norm cancellation, since the supports meet only in the small set.”
Lean: [Theorem1.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Theorem1.lean:373) uses only `Zvol_le` and `nrm_add_le`; it does not prove minimal fillings have separated supports or no cancellation.
Verdict: OVERCLAIM.
Correction: “Adding minimal fillings gives a filling of `X+Y`, and subadditivity `nrm_add_le` gives `Zvol (X+Y) ≤ Zvol X + Zvol Y`.”

4. [notes/taut-lean-proof.tex](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:241)
Claim: extreme-hybrid opposite faces form a cycle “closed by the same `F_2`-parity behind Lemma cut.”
Lean: [Splitting.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Splitting.lean:286) `no_extreme_hybrid` is a coefficient-level integer boundary/cone argument and does not invoke `range_bd2_eq_ker_bd1` or `exists_cut`.
Verdict: WRONG-CITATION.
Correction: “their link chain is closed by the coefficient-level boundary vanishing in `no_extreme_hybrid`, yielding a forbidden complete cone.”

**Confirmed Faithful**

Definitions 1.1-1.6 match the Lean objects, with the caveat above for the cone corollary prose. The two calibrated fixes are correct: additivity now uses inclusive `Kkills_or_Kkills`, and prime side splitting is no longer described as an `IsTaut.splits` invocation. Theorem 2.4 has `hn : 2 ≤ n`; additivity has `hn : 1 ≤ n`. `taut_isPseudomanifold` proves only `IsPseudomanifold` triangle-boundedness; purity comes separately from `IsTaut.dim_pure`. Section 5’s clean-complex bridge is faithful, and there is no Lean theorem bridging `IsStickerball` to a topological/PL 3-ball.
